2. TypeScript Nexus Engine with Enhanced Ollama Integration
Now, let me create the TypeScript service with robust Ollama API integration:
typescript
// services/nexusEngine.ts
import axios, { AxiosError, AxiosResponse } from 'axios';
import { config } from '../config';
// Types
interface OllamaOptions {
temperature?: number;
num_predict?: number;
top_p?: number;
repeat_penalty?: number;
seed?: number;
[key: string]: any;
}
interface OllamaRequest {
model: string;
prompt: string;
stream?: boolean;
options?: OllamaOptions;
}
interface OllamaResponse {
model: string;
response: string;
done: boolean;
context?: number[];
total_duration?: number;
load_duration?: number;
prompt_eval_count?: number;
eval_count?: number;
}
interface AgentResponse {
agent: string;
decision: string;
reasoning: string;
confidence: number;
rawResponse: string;
}
interface OllamaError {
type: 'connection' | 'timeout' | 'model_not_found' | 'api_error' | 'rate_limit' | 'parsing';
message: string;
details?: any;
}
// Configuration
const OLLAMA_HOST = process.env.OLLAMA_HOST || 'http://localhost:11434';
const OLLAMA_TIMEOUT = parseInt(process.env.OLLAMA_TIMEOUT || '30000');
const OLLAMA_RETRY_ATTEMPTS = parseInt(process.env.OLLAMA_RETRY_ATTEMPTS || '2');
const GEMINI_API_KEY = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || config.geminiApiKey;
const FALLBACK_ENABLED = process.env.FALLBACK_ENABLED !== 'false';
// Model cache
let availableModels: string[] = [];
let lastModelDiscovery = 0;
const MODEL_CACHE_TTL = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes
/**
* Enhanced Ollama API client with robust error handling
*/
export class OllamaBridge {
private static instance: OllamaBridge;
static getInstance(): OllamaBridge {
if (!OllamaBridge.instance) {
OllamaBridge.instance = new OllamaBridge();
}
return OllamaBridge.instance;
}
/**
* Query Ollama with enhanced error handling and fallback
*/
async queryOllama(
prompt: string,
model: string = 'llama3',
options: OllamaOptions = {},
stream: boolean = false
): Promise {
const requestData: OllamaRequest = {
model,
prompt,
stream,
options: {
temperature: 0.7,
num_predict: 512,
...options
}
};
// Log the attempt
console.log(`[OllamaBridge] Querying model ${model} with ${prompt.length} chars`);
try {
const response = await this.makeRequestWithRetry(
`${OLLAMA_HOST}/api/generate`,
requestData,
OLLAMA_RETRY_ATTEMPTS
);
return this.parseResponse(response, model, stream);
} catch (error) {
const ollamaError = this.classifyError(error);
// Enhanced error logging
console.error(`[OllamaBridge] Query failed:`, {
errorType: ollamaError.type,
message: ollamaError.message,
model,
promptLength: prompt.length,
timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
});
// If fallback is enabled and this is a recoverable error
if (FALLBACK_ENABLED && this.isRecoverableError(ollamaError)) {
console.warn(`[OllamaBridge] Attempting fallback to Gemini API`);
return await this.fallbackToGemini(prompt, model, ollamaError);
}
throw new Error(`Ollama query failed: ${ollamaError.message}`);
}
}
/**
* Make HTTP request with retry logic
*/
private async makeRequestWithRetry(
url: string,
data: OllamaRequest,
retries: number
): Promise {
for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= retries; attempt++) {
try {
const response = await axios.post(url, data, {
timeout: OLLAMA_TIMEOUT,
headers: {
'Content-Type': 'application/json'
},
validateStatus: (status) => status === 200
});
return response;
} catch (error) {
const axiosError = error as AxiosError;
// Log retry attempts
if (attempt < retries) {
const delay = Math.pow(2, attempt) * 1000; // Exponential backoff
console.warn(`[OllamaBridge] Attempt ${attempt + 1} failed, retrying in ${delay}ms:`,
axiosError.message);
await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, delay));
continue;
}
// Last attempt failed
throw error;
}
}
throw new Error('Max retry attempts exceeded');
}
/**
* Classify Ollama errors for better handling
*/
private classifyError(error: any): OllamaError {
const axiosError = error as AxiosError;
if (axiosError.code === 'ECONNREFUSED') {
return {
type: 'connection',
message: 'Cannot connect to Ollama. Ensure it is running at the correct host/port.',
details: { host: OLLAMA_HOST }
};
}
if (axiosError.code === 'ETIMEDOUT') {
return {
type: 'timeout',
message: `Ollama request timed out after ${OLLAMA_TIMEOUT}ms.`,
details: { timeout: OLLAMA_TIMEOUT }
};
}
if (axiosError.response?.status === 404) {
return {
type: 'model_not_found',
message: `Model not found. Please ensure the model is pulled: 'ollama pull '`,
details: { status: 404 }
};
}
if (axiosError.response?.status === 429) {
return {
type: 'rate_limit',
message: 'Ollama rate limit exceeded. Please try again later.',
details: { status: 429 }
};
}
// Try to extract error from response body
if (axiosError.response?.data) {
try {
const errorData = typeof axiosError.response.data === 'string'
? JSON.parse(axiosError.response.data)
: axiosError.response.data;
return {
type: 'api_error',
message: errorData.error || 'Ollama API error',
details: errorData
};
} catch {
// If we can't parse, return generic error
}
}
// Default parsing error
if (error.message?.includes('Unexpected token') || error.message?.includes('JSON')) {
return {
type: 'parsing',
message: 'Failed to parse Ollama response. The response format may have changed.',
details: { rawResponse: error.message }
};
}
return {
type: 'api_error',
message: `Ollama error: ${error.message || 'Unknown error'}`,
details: error
};
}
/**
* Check if error is recoverable (should trigger fallback)
*/
private isRecoverableError(error: OllamaError): boolean {
const recoverableTypes: OllamaError['type'][] = [
'connection',
'timeout',
'model_not_found',
'api_error'
];
return recoverableTypes.includes(error.type) && !!GEMINI_API_KEY;
}
/**
* Fallback to Gemini API
*/
private async fallbackToGemini(
prompt: string,
originalModel: string,
ollamaError: OllamaError
): Promise {
try {
console.log(`[OllamaBridge] Starting fallback to Gemini API. Original error: ${ollamaError.message}`);
const fallbackPrompt = this.createFallbackPrompt(prompt, originalModel, ollamaError);
const response = await axios.post(
`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-pro:generateContent`,
{
contents: [{
parts: [{ text: fallbackPrompt }]
}]
},
{
headers: {
'Content-Type': 'application/json',
'x-goog-api-key': GEMINI_API_KEY
},
timeout: 30000
}
);
const geminiText = response.data?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;
if (!geminiText) {
throw new Error('Gemini API returned empty response');
}
// Log successful fallback
console.log(`[OllamaBridge] Fallback to Gemini successful. Response length: ${geminiText.length} chars`);
// Return formatted as Ollama response for compatibility
return {
model: 'gemini-pro (fallback)',
response: geminiText,
done: true,
context: []
};
} catch (fallbackError) {
console.error(`[OllamaBridge] Gemini fallback also failed:`, fallbackError);
throw new Error(`Both Ollama and Gemini failed: ${ollamaError.message}`);
}
}
/**
* Create enhanced prompt for fallback
*/
private createFallbackPrompt(
originalPrompt: string,
originalModel: string,
error: OllamaError
): string {
return `
[Context: This query was originally intended for local Ollama model "${originalModel}"
but failed due to: ${error.message}]
[Instructions: Please provide a response as if you were the local AI model.
Maintain the expected response format and tone.]
Original query: ${originalPrompt}
`;
}
/**
* Parse Ollama response with robust handling
*/
private parseResponse(
response: AxiosResponse,
model: string,
stream: boolean
): OllamaResponse {
try {
if (stream) {
return this.parseStreamResponse(response.data, model);
} else {
return this.parseStandardResponse(response.data, model);
}
} catch (error) {
console.error(`[OllamaBridge] Failed to parse response:`, error);
// Attempt to extract meaningful information even if parsing fails
const rawData = response.data;
let extractedResponse = '';
if (typeof rawData === 'string') {
// Try to find JSON-like structure
const jsonMatch = rawData.match(/"response":\s*"([^"]+)"/);
if (jsonMatch) {
extractedResponse = jsonMatch[1];
} else {
// Try to extract any text that looks like a response
extractedResponse = rawData.substring(0, 500);
}
} else if (rawData && typeof rawData === 'object') {
// Try various known response formats
extractedResponse =
rawData.response ||
rawData.text ||
rawData.content ||
JSON.stringify(rawData).substring(0, 500);
}
return {
model,
response: extractedResponse || 'Unable to parse response',
done: true,
context: []
};
}
}
/**
* Parse standard (non-streaming) response
*/
private parseStandardResponse(data: any, model: string): OllamaResponse {
// Handle various response formats
let responseText = '';
let done = true;
if (typeof data === 'string') {
// Try to parse as JSON
try {
const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
responseText = parsed.response || parsed.text || '';
done = parsed.done !== false;
} catch {
// If not JSON, use as-is
responseText = data;
}
} else if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
// Handle different possible structures
if (data.response !== undefined) {
responseText = data.response;
done = data.done !== false;
} else if (data.text !== undefined) {
responseText = data.text;
} else if (data.content !== undefined) {
responseText = data.content;
} else {
// Last resort: stringify
responseText = JSON.stringify(data);
}
}
return {
model,
response: responseText,
done,
context: data.context || [],
total_duration: data.total_duration,
load_duration: data.load_duration,
prompt_eval_count: data.prompt_eval_count,
eval_count: data.eval_count
};
}
/**
* Parse streaming response
*/
private parseStreamResponse(data: any, model: string): OllamaResponse {
// Streaming responses come as newline-delimited JSON
const lines = data.split('\n').filter((line: string) => line.trim());
let fullResponse = '';
let context: number[] = [];
for (const line of lines) {
try {
const parsed = JSON.parse(line);
if (parsed.response) {
fullResponse += parsed.response;
}
if (parsed.done && parsed.context) {
context = parsed.context;
}
} catch {
// Skip invalid JSON lines
}
}
return {
model,
response: fullResponse,
done: true,
context
};
}
/**
* Discover available Ollama models
*/
async discoverModels(forceRefresh: boolean = false): Promise {
const now = Date.now();
// Use cache if valid
if (!forceRefresh && availableModels.length > 0 &&
(now - lastModelDiscovery) < MODEL_CACHE_TTL) {
return availableModels;
}
try {
const response = await axios.get(`${OLLAMA_HOST}/api/tags`, {
timeout: 5000
});
if (response.data?.models) {
availableModels = response.data.models.map((model: any) => model.name);
lastModelDiscovery = now;
console.log(`[OllamaBridge] Discovered ${availableModels.length} models`);
return availableModels;
}
return [];
} catch (error) {
console.error(`[OllamaBridge] Failed to discover models:`, error);
return [];
}
}
/**
* Test Ollama connection and model
*/
async testConnection(model: string = 'llama3'): Promise<{
success: boolean;
message: string;
details?: any;
}> {
try {
// Test connectivity
await axios.get(`${OLLAMA_HOST}/api/tags`, { timeout: 3000 });
// Test model
const response = await this.queryOllama(
'Please respond with the word "READY" if you are operational.',
model,
{ temperature: 0.1, num_predict: 10 },
false
);
const isReady = response.response?.toUpperCase().includes('READY');
return {
success: isReady,
message: isReady
? `Model ${model} is ready and responding`
: `Model responded but not as expected: ${response.response}`,
details: {
model,
response: response.response,
responseTime: response.total_duration
}
};
} catch (error) {
const classifiedError = this.classifyError(error);
return {
success: false,
message: `Connection test failed: ${classifiedError.message}`,
details: classifiedError
};
}
}
}
/**
* Enhanced agent response parser
*/
export class AgentResponseParser {
/**
* Parse agent response with multiple fallback strategies
*/
static parseAgentResponse(
rawResponse: string,
expectedAgent: string
): AgentResponse {
const strategies = [
this.parseStructuredJson,
this.parseMarkdownWithHeaders,
this.parseKeyValueFormat,
this.parseNaturalLanguage
];
for (const strategy of strategies) {
try {
const parsed = strategy(rawResponse, expectedAgent);
if (parsed && parsed.decision && parsed.reasoning) {
return {
...parsed,
rawResponse,
confidence: parsed.confidence || this.calculateConfidence(parsed)
};
}
} catch {
// Try next strategy
continue;
}
}
// Last resort fallback
return this.createFallbackResponse(rawResponse, expectedAgent);
}
/**
* Strategy 1: Parse structured JSON
*/
private static parseStructuredJson(
rawResponse: string,
expectedAgent: string
): Partial | null {
// Try to find JSON in the response
const jsonMatch = rawResponse.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
if (!jsonMatch) return null;
try {
const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);
// Handle various JSON structures
return {
agent: parsed.agent || parsed.role || expectedAgent,
decision: parsed.decision || parsed.result || parsed.output || '',
reasoning: parsed.reasoning || parsed.analysis || parsed.explanation || '',
confidence: parsed.confidence || parsed.certainty || 0.5
};
} catch {
return null;
}
}
/**
* Strategy 2: Parse markdown with headers
*/
private static parseMarkdownWithHeaders(
rawResponse: string,
expectedAgent: string
): Partial | null {
const decisionMatch = rawResponse.match(/##? Decision[:\s]*\n*(.*?)(?=\n##? |$)/is);
const reasoningMatch = rawResponse.match(/##? Reasoning[:\s]*\n*(.*?)(?=\n##? |$)/is);
const agentMatch = rawResponse.match(/##? Agent[:\s]*\n*(\w+)/i);
if (!decisionMatch && !reasoningMatch) return null;
return {
agent: agentMatch?.[1] || expectedAgent,
decision: decisionMatch?.[1]?.trim() || '',
reasoning: reasoningMatch?.[1]?.trim() || '',
confidence: this.extractConfidenceFromText(rawResponse)
};
}
/**
* Strategy 3: Parse key-value format
*/
private static parseKeyValueFormat(
rawResponse: string,
expectedAgent: string
): Partial | null {
const lines = rawResponse.split('\n');
let decision = '';
let reasoning = '';
let agent = expectedAgent;
for (const line of lines) {
const lowerLine = line.toLowerCase();
if (lowerLine.includes('decision:')) {
decision = line.split(':').slice(1).join(':').trim();
} else if (lowerLine.includes('reasoning:')) {
reasoning = line.split(':').slice(1).join(':').trim();
} else if (lowerLine.includes('agent:')) {
agent = line.split(':').slice(1).join(':').trim();
}
}
if (!decision && !reasoning) return null;
return {
agent,
decision,
reasoning,
confidence: this.extractConfidenceFromText(rawResponse)
};
}
/**
* Strategy 4: Parse natural language (last resort)
*/
private static parseNaturalLanguage(
rawResponse: string,
expectedAgent: string
): Partial | null {
// Extract first sentence as decision, rest as reasoning
const sentences = rawResponse.split(/[.!?]+/).filter(s => s.trim());
if (sentences.length === 0) return null;
return {
agent: expectedAgent,
decision: sentences[0].trim(),
reasoning: sentences.slice(1).join('. ').trim() || sentences[0].trim(),
confidence: 0.3 // Low confidence for this method
};
}
/**
* Extract confidence from text using various patterns
*/
private static extractConfidenceFromText(text: string): number {
const patterns = [
/confidence[:\s]*(\d+\.?\d*)/i,
/certainty[:\s]*(\d+\.?\d*)/i,
/(\d+)% (confidence|certainty)/i,
/I am (\d+)% sure/i
];
for (const pattern of patterns) {
const match = text.match(pattern);
if (match) {
const value = parseFloat(match[1]);
if (!isNaN(value)) {
return value > 1 ? value / 100 : value;
}
}
}
// Default medium confidence
return 0.5;
}
/**
* Calculate confidence based on response characteristics
*/
private static calculateConfidence(parsed: Partial): number {
let confidence = 0.5;
// Longer reasoning suggests more confidence
if (parsed.reasoning && parsed.reasoning.length > 50) {
confidence += 0.2;
}
// Clear decision suggests more confidence
if (parsed.decision && parsed.decision.length > 10) {
confidence += 0.2;
}
// Contains numbers or specific references
if (parsed.decision?.match(/\d+/) || parsed.reasoning?.match(/\d+/)) {
confidence += 0.1;
}
return Math.min(0.95, confidence);
}
/**
* Create fallback response when all parsing fails
*/
private static createFallbackResponse(
rawResponse: string,
expectedAgent: string
): AgentResponse {
return {
agent: expectedAgent,
decision: 'Unable to parse specific decision',
reasoning: `Raw response: ${rawResponse.substring(0, 200)}...`,
confidence: 0.1,
rawResponse
};
}
}
/**
* Enhanced Nexus Engine with multi-agent collaboration
*/
export class NexusEngine {
private ollamaBridge: OllamaBridge;
private activeModel: string = 'llama3';
constructor() {
this.ollamaBridge = OllamaBridge.getInstance();
}
/**
* Process prompt with multi-agent collaboration
*/
async processPrompt(
prompt: string,
options: {
model?: string;
useAgents?: string[];
enableStreaming?: boolean;
} = {}
): Promise<{
success: boolean;
result: any;
agentDecisions: AgentResponse[];
fallbackUsed: boolean;
errors: string[];
}> {
const startTime = Date.now();
const errors: string[] = [];
let fallbackUsed = false;
// Set active model
if (options.model) {
this.activeModel = options.model;
}
// Determine which agents to use
const agents = options.useAgents || ['CUBE', 'CORE', 'LOOP', 'WAVE', 'SIGN', 'LINE', 'COIN', 'WORK'];
console.log(`[NexusEngine] Processing prompt with ${agents.length} agents`);
try {
// Phase 1: Individual agent analysis
const agentPromises = agents.map(agent =>
this.executeAgent(agent, prompt, options.enableStreaming)
);
const agentResults = await Promise.allSettled(agentPromises);
// Process agent results
const successfulAgents: AgentResponse[] = [];
const agentDecisions: AgentResponse[] = [];
agentResults.forEach((result, index) => {
const agent = agents[index];
if (result.status === 'fulfilled') {
const agentResponse = AgentResponseParser.parseAgentResponse(
result.value,
agent
);
successfulAgents.push(agentResponse);
agentDecisions.push(agentResponse);
console.log(`[NexusEngine] Agent ${agent} completed with confidence ${agentResponse.confidence}`);
} else {
errors.push(`Agent ${agent} failed: ${result.reason}`);
console.error(`[NexusEngine] Agent ${agent} failed:`, result.reason);
}
});
// Phase 2: Agent collaboration (if we have multiple successful agents)
let collaborationResult = '';
if (successfulAgents.length > 1) {
collaborationResult = await this.coordinateAgents(
prompt,
successfulAgents,
options.enableStreaming
);
} else if (successfulAgents.length === 1) {
collaborationResult = successfulAgents[0].decision;
} else {
throw new Error('All agents failed to process the prompt');
}
// Phase 3: Final synthesis
const finalResult = await this.synthesizeResult(
prompt,
collaborationResult,
agentDecisions,
options.enableStreaming
);
const processingTime = Date.now() - startTime;
console.log(`[NexusEngine] Processing completed in ${processingTime}ms`);
console.log(`[NexusEngine] ${successfulAgents.length}/${agents.length} agents successful`);
if (fallbackUsed) {
console.warn(`[NexusEngine] Fallback mechanism was used`);
}
return {
success: true,
result: finalResult,
agentDecisions,
fallbackUsed,
errors
};
} catch (error) {
const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
errors.push(`Processing failed: ${errorMessage}`);
console.error(`[NexusEngine] Processing failed:`, error);
// Final fallback: direct query without agents
try {
console.warn(`[NexusEngine] Attempting direct query as final fallback`);
const directResponse = await this.ollamaBridge.queryOllama(
prompt,
this.activeModel,
{ temperature: 0.7 }
);
fallbackUsed = true;
return {
success: true,
result: directResponse.response,
agentDecisions: [],
fallbackUsed: true,
errors: [...errors, 'Used direct query fallback']
};
} catch (fallbackError) {
return {
success: false,
result: 'All processing attempts failed',
agentDecisions: [],
fallbackUsed: false,
errors: [...errors, `Fallback also failed: ${fallbackError}`]
};
}
}
}
/**
* Execute individual agent
*/
private async executeAgent(
agent: string,
prompt: string,
stream?: boolean
): Promise {
const agentPrompt = this.createAgentPrompt(agent, prompt);
const response = await this.ollamaBridge.queryOllama(
agentPrompt,
this.activeModel,
this.getAgentOptions(agent),
stream
);
return response.response;
}
/**
* Create agent-specific prompt
*/
private createAgentPrompt(agent: string, prompt: string): string {
const agentInstructions: Record = {
CUBE: 'Analyze the structure and components. Focus on architecture and organization.',
CORE: 'Identify the core logic and truth. Focus on reasoning and principles.',
LOOP: 'Look for patterns and optimization opportunities. Focus on iterations and improvements.',
WAVE: 'Analyze flow, trends, and dynamics. Focus on changes and variability.',
SIGN: 'Interpret symbols and hidden meanings. Focus on semiotics and interpretation.',
LINE: 'Connect sequences and progressions. Focus on linear flow and connections.',
COIN: 'Assess value, risk, and probability. Focus on cost-benefit analysis.',
WORK: 'Create actionable steps and implementation plans. Focus on execution.',
CODE: 'Generate executable code based on insights. Focus on practical implementation.',
SYNC: 'Coordinate and synthesize multiple perspectives. Focus on integration.'
};
return `
[ROLE: ${agent}]
${agentInstructions[agent] || 'Analyze from your specialized perspective.'}
TASK: ${prompt}
Please provide:
1. Your analysis and decision
2. Your reasoning process
3. Any specific recommendations or next steps
Format your response clearly, using sections if helpful.
`;
}
/**
* Get agent-specific options
*/
private getAgentOptions(agent: string): OllamaOptions {
const baseOptions: OllamaOptions = {
temperature: 0.7,
num_predict: 512
};
// Adjust based on agent type
switch (agent) {
case 'CUBE':
case 'CORE':
return { ...baseOptions, temperature: 0.3, num_predict: 768 };
case 'LOOP':
case 'CODE':
return { ...baseOptions, temperature: 0.5, num_predict: 1024 };
case 'SIGN':
case 'WAVE':
return { ...baseOptions, temperature: 0.8, num_predict: 512 };
default:
return baseOptions;
}
}
/**
* Coordinate multiple agents
*/
private async coordinateAgents(
originalPrompt: string,
agentResults: AgentResponse[],
stream?: boolean
): Promise {
const coordinationPrompt = this.createCoordinationPrompt(
originalPrompt,
agentResults
);
const response = await this.ollamaBridge.queryOllama(
coordinationPrompt,
this.activeModel,
{ temperature: 0.5, num_predict: 1024 },
stream
);
return response.response;
}
/**
* Create coordination prompt
*/
private createCoordinationPrompt(
originalPrompt: string,
agentResults: AgentResponse[]
): string {
let agentContributions = '';
agentResults.forEach(result => {
agentContributions += `
=== ${result.agent} (confidence: ${result.confidence.toFixed(2)}) ===
Decision: ${result.decision}
Reasoning: ${result.reasoning}
`;
});
return `
[ROLE: SYNCHRONIZER]
You are coordinating insights from multiple specialized agents.
ORIGINAL TASK: ${originalPrompt}
AGENT CONTRIBUTIONS:
${agentContributions}
YOUR TASK:
1. Synthesize the key insights from all agents
2. Identify areas of agreement and disagreement
3. Provide integrated recommendations
4. Highlight the most confident insights
Please provide a comprehensive coordination report.
`;
}
/**
* Synthesize final result
*/
private async synthesizeResult(
originalPrompt: string,
coordinationResult: string,
agentDecisions: AgentResponse[],
stream?: boolean
): Promise {
const synthesisPrompt = `
[ROLE: FINAL SYNTHESIZER]
Create the final output based on the coordinated analysis.
ORIGINAL REQUEST: ${originalPrompt}
COORDINATED ANALYSIS: ${coordinationResult}
AGENT DECISIONS SUMMARY:
${agentDecisions.map(a => `- ${a.agent}: ${a.decision.substring(0, 100)}...`).join('\n')}
Create a final response that:
1. Directly addresses the original request
2. Incorporates the coordinated insights
3. Is clear, actionable, and well-structured
4. Highlights key takeaways and next steps
`;
const response = await this.ollamaBridge.queryOllama(
synthesisPrompt,
this.activeModel,
{ temperature: 0.4, num_predict: 768 },
stream
);
return response.response;
}
/**
* Get available models
*/
async getAvailableModels(forceRefresh: boolean = false): Promise {
return this.ollamaBridge.discoverModels(forceRefresh);
}
/**
* Set active model
*/
setActiveModel(model: string): void {
this.activeModel = model;
console.log(`[NexusEngine] Active model set to: ${model}`);
}
/**
* Get active model
*/
getActiveModel(): string {
return this.activeModel;
}
/**
* Test Ollama connection
*/
async testConnection(model?: string): Promise<{
success: boolean;
message: string;
details?: any;
}> {
return this.ollamaBridge.testConnection(model || this.activeModel);
}
}
// Export singleton instance
export const nexusEngine = new NexusEngine();

Now, let me create the enhanced React components for the UI integration:
typescript
// components/OllamaModelSelector.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { nexusEngine } from '../services/nexusEngine';
interface OllamaModelSelectorProps {
onModelChange: (model: string) => void;
currentModel: string;
showStatus?: boolean;
}
const OllamaModelSelector: React.FC = ({
onModelChange,
currentModel,
showStatus = true
}) => {
const [models, setModels] = useState([]);
const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
const [error, setError] = useState(null);
const [connectionStatus, setConnectionStatus] = useState<'checking' | 'connected' | 'disconnected' | 'error'>('checking');
useEffect(() => {
loadModels();
checkConnection();
}, []);
const loadModels = async () => {
setLoading(true);
setError(null);
try {
const availableModels = await nexusEngine.getAvailableModels(true);
setModels(availableModels);
if (availableModels.length === 0) {
setError('No Ollama models found. Make sure Ollama is running and models are pulled.');
}
} catch (err) {
setError('Failed to load models. Check Ollama connection.');
console.error('Failed to load models:', err);
} finally {
setLoading(false);
}
};
const checkConnection = async () => {
setConnectionStatus('checking');
try {
const result = await nexusEngine.testConnection();
setConnectionStatus(result.success ? 'connected' : 'disconnected');
if (!result.success) {
setError(`Connection failed: ${result.message}`);
}
} catch {
setConnectionStatus('error');
setError('Failed to check connection');
}
};
const handleModelSelect = (model: string) => {
nexusEngine.setActiveModel(model);
onModelChange(model);
};
const handleRefresh = () => {
loadModels();
checkConnection();
};
const getStatusColor = () => {
switch (connectionStatus) {
case 'connected': return 'bg-green-500';
case 'disconnected': return 'bg-yellow-500';
case 'error': return 'bg-red-500';
case 'checking': return 'bg-blue-500';
default: return 'bg-gray-500';
}
};
const getStatusText = () => {
switch (connectionStatus) {
case 'connected': return 'Connected';
case 'disconnected': return 'Ollama running, model issues';
case 'error': return 'Connection failed';
case 'checking': return 'Checking...';
default: return 'Unknown';
}
};
return (
Ollama Model
onClick={handleRefresh}
disabled={loading}
className="px-3 py-1 text-sm bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 rounded hover:bg-blue-200 disabled:opacity-50"
>
{loading ? 'Loading...' : 'Refresh'}
{showStatus && (
{getStatusText()}
{currentModel && (
Active: {currentModel}
)}
)}
{error && (
{error}
)}
Select Model
value={currentModel}
onChange={(e) => handleModelSelect(e.target.value)}
disabled={loading || models.length === 0}
className="w-full p-2 border rounded bg-white disabled:opacity-50"
>
{loading ? (
Loading models...
) : models.length === 0 ? (
No models available
) : (
<>
Select a model
{models.map((model) => (
{model} {model === currentModel && '(active)'}
))}
)}
{models.length > 0 && (
Available Models:
{models.map((model) => (
key={model}
className={`px-2 py-1 rounded ${
model === currentModel
? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700'
: 'hover:bg-gray-100'
}`}
onClick={() => handleModelSelect(model)}
>
{model}
))}
)}
Ollama Host: {process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_OLLAMA_HOST || 'http://localhost:11434'}
Fallback to Gemini: {process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GEMINI_API_KEY ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}
);
};
export default OllamaModelSelector;

// components/StatusSidebar.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { nexusEngine } from '../services/nexusEngine';
interface StatusSidebarProps {
className?: string;
}
interface SystemStatus {
ollamaConnected: boolean;
activeModel: string;
modelCount: number;
lastChecked: Date;
errors: string[];
}
const StatusSidebar: React.FC = ({ className = '' }) => {
const [status, setStatus] = useState({
ollamaConnected: false,
activeModel: '',
modelCount: 0,
lastChecked: new Date(),
errors: []
});
const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
const [showDetails, setShowDetails] = useState(false);
useEffect(() => {
checkStatus();
const interval = setInterval(checkStatus, 30000); // Check every 30 seconds
return () => clearInterval(interval);
}, []);
const checkStatus = async () => {
setLoading(true);
try {
const [models, connectionTest, activeModel] = await Promise.all([
nexusEngine.getAvailableModels(),
nexusEngine.testConnection(),
Promise.resolve(nexusEngine.getActiveModel())
]);
setStatus({
ollamaConnected: connectionTest.success,
activeModel,
modelCount: models.length,
lastChecked: new Date(),
errors: connectionTest.success ? [] : [connectionTest.message]
});
} catch (error) {
setStatus(prev => ({
...prev,
ollamaConnected: false,
errors: [...prev.errors, `Status check failed: ${error}`],
lastChecked: new Date()
}));
} finally {
setLoading(false);
}
};
const formatTime = (date: Date) => {
return date.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
};
const getConnectionStatus = () => {
if (loading) return 'Checking...';
return status.ollamaConnected ? 'Connected' : 'Disconnected';
};
const getStatusColor = () => {
if (loading) return 'bg-yellow-500';
return status.ollamaConnected ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-red-500';
};
return (
System Status
onClick={() => setShowDetails(!showDetails)}
className="text-sm text-gray-400 hover:text-white"
>
{showDetails ? 'Hide Details' : 'Show Details'}
{getConnectionStatus()}
{formatTime(status.lastChecked)}
{status.activeModel && (
Active Model
{status.activeModel}
)}
Available Models
{status.modelCount}
{showDetails && (
Ollama Host
{process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_OLLAMA_HOST || 'http://localhost:11434'}
Fallback Status
{process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GEMINI_API_KEY
? 'Gemini fallback enabled'
: 'Gemini fallback disabled'}
{status.errors.length > 0 && (
Recent Errors
{status.errors.map((error, index) => (
{error}
))}
)}
onClick={checkStatus}
disabled={loading}
className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded disabled:opacity-50"
>
{loading ? 'Checking...' : 'Check Status Now'}
)}
Agent System v5.0
Multi-Agent Collaboration Enabled
);
};
export default StatusSidebar;
typescript
// components/CollaborationVisualizer.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { AgentResponse } from '../services/nexusEngine';
interface CollaborationVisualizerProps {
agentDecisions: AgentResponse[];
activeModel: string;
}
const CollaborationVisualizer: React.FC = ({
agentDecisions,
activeModel
}) => {
const getAgentColor = (agent: string) => {
const colors: Record = {
CUBE: 'bg-blue-500',
CORE: 'bg-purple-500',
LOOP: 'bg-green-500',
WAVE: 'bg-teal-500',
SIGN: 'bg-yellow-500',
LINE: 'bg-orange-500',
COIN: 'bg-red-500',
WORK: 'bg-pink-500',
CODE: 'bg-indigo-500',
SYNC: 'bg-gray-500'
};
return colors[agent] || 'bg-gray-400';
};
const getAgentIcon = (agent: string) => {
const icons: Record = {
CUBE: '⬢',
CORE: '⚡',
LOOP: '↻',
WAVE: '〰️',
SIGN: '🔣',
LINE: '➡️',
COIN: '🪙',
WORK: '🛠️',
CODE: '💻',
SYNC: '🔄'
};
return icons[agent] || '🤖';
};
const communicationLines = [
{ from: 'SIGN', to: 'CODE', label: 'symbol interpretation' },
{ from: 'LOOP', to: 'CODE', label: 'optimization patterns' },
{ from: 'CUBE', to: 'WORK', label: 'structure analysis' },
{ from: 'COIN', to: 'WORK', label: 'risk assessment' },
{ from: 'CORE', to: 'SYNC', label: 'logic synthesis' }
];
if (agentDecisions.length === 0) {
return (
🤖
No agent collaboration data yet
Agents will appear here as they process
);
}
return (
Agent Collaboration
Model: {activeModel}
{/* Communication Network Visualization */}
{agentDecisions.map((agent, index) => {
const row = Math.floor(index / 3) + 1;
const col = (index % 3) + 1;
return (
key={agent.agent}
className={`absolute ${getAgentColor(agent.agent)} text-white rounded-lg p-3 flex flex-col items-center justify-center shadow-lg`}
style={{
top: `${row * 25}%`,
left: `${col * 25}%`,
transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)',
width: '80px',
height: '80px'
}}
>
{getAgentIcon(agent.agent)}
{agent.agent}
{Math.round(agent.confidence * 100)}%
);
})}
{/* Communication lines */}
{communicationLines.map((line, index) => {
const fromAgent = agentDecisions.find(a => a.agent === line.from);
const toAgent = agentDecisions.find(a => a.agent === line.to);
if (!fromAgent || !toAgent) return null;
const fromIndex = agentDecisions.findIndex(a => a.agent === line.from);
const toIndex = agentDecisions.findIndex(a => a.agent === line.to);
const fromRow = Math.floor(fromIndex / 3) + 1;
const fromCol = (fromIndex % 3) + 1;
const toRow = Math.floor(toIndex / 3) + 1;
const toCol = (toIndex % 3) + 1;
const x1 = `${(fromCol * 25)}%`;
const y1 = `${(fromRow * 25)}%`;
const x2 = `${(toCol * 25)}%`;
const y2 = `${(toRow * 25)}%`;
return (
x1={x1}
y1={y1}
x2={x2}
y2={y2}
stroke="#9CA3AF"
strokeWidth="2"
strokeDasharray="5,5"
/>
x={`${(parseFloat(x1) + parseFloat(x2)) / 2}%`}
y={`${(parseFloat(y1) + parseFloat(y2)) / 2 - 10}%`}
textAnchor="middle"
className="text-xs fill-gray-600"
>
{line.label}
);
})}
{/* Agent Decisions */}
Agent Decisions
{agentDecisions.map((agent) => (
key={agent.agent}
className={`p-4 rounded-lg border ${getAgentColor(agent.agent).replace('bg-', 'border-').replace('500', '200')} bg-white`}
>
{getAgentIcon(agent.agent)}
{agent.agent}
{Math.round(agent.confidence * 100)}% confidence
Decision
{agent.decision.substring(0, 100)}...
Reasoning
{agent.reasoning.substring(0, 150)}...
Communication: {communicationLines
.filter(line => line.from === agent.agent || line.to === agent.agent)
.map(line => line.from === agent.agent ? `→ ${line.to}` : `← ${line.from}`)
.join(', ') || 'None'}
))}
);
};
export default CollaborationVisualizer;
4. Enhanced App.tsx Integration

// App.tsx (enhanced version)
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import OllamaModelSelector from './components/OllamaModelSelector';
import StatusSidebar from './components/StatusSidebar';
import CollaborationVisualizer from './components/CollaborationVisualizer';
import { nexusEngine, AgentResponse } from './services/nexusEngine';
const App: React.FC = () => {
const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('');
const [response, setResponse] = useState('');
const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
const [activeModel, setActiveModel] = useState('llama3');
const [agentDecisions, setAgentDecisions] = useState([]);
const [errors, setErrors] = useState([]);
const [fallbackUsed, setFallbackUsed] = useState(false);
const [showCollaboration, setShowCollaboration] = useState(true);
const [streaming, setStreaming] = useState(false);
useEffect(() => {
// Initialize with current model
setActiveModel(nexusEngine.getActiveModel());
}, []);
const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
e.preventDefault();
if (!prompt.trim()) return;
setLoading(true);
setResponse('');
setAgentDecisions([]);
setErrors([]);
setFallbackUsed(false);
console.log(`[App] Processing prompt with model: ${activeModel}`);
try {
const result = await nexusEngine.processPrompt(prompt, {
model: activeModel,
enableStreaming: streaming
});
setResponse(result.result);
setAgentDecisions(result.agentDecisions);
setErrors(result.errors);
setFallbackUsed(result.fallbackUsed);
// Log fallback event with details
if (result.fallbackUsed) {
console.warn(`[App] Fallback mechanism was triggered. Details:`, {
originalModel: activeModel,
errors: result.errors,
timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
});
}
} catch (error) {
const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
setErrors([...errors, `Processing failed: ${errorMessage}`]);
console.error('[App] Processing failed:', error);
} finally {
setLoading(false);
}
};
const handleModelChange = (model: string) => {
setActiveModel(model);
console.log(`[App] Model changed to: ${model}`);
};
return (
NEXUS AI Orchestrator v5.0
Multi-Agent AI System with Ollama Integration & Collaboration
{/* Left Panel */}
{/* Model Selector */}
onModelChange={handleModelChange}
currentModel={activeModel}
showStatus={true}
/>
{/* Input Form */}
Enter your prompt for multi-agent analysis
value={prompt}
onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
rows={6}
className="w-full p-4 border rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500"
placeholder="Describe your task for the AI agents..."
disabled={loading}
/>
type="submit"
disabled={loading || !prompt.trim()}
className="px-6 py-3 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
>
{loading ? 'Processing...' : 'Analyze with Agents'}
type="checkbox"
checked={streaming}
onChange={(e) => setStreaming(e.target.checked)}
className="rounded"
/>
Enable streaming
type="button"
onClick={() => setShowCollaboration(!showCollaboration)}
className="px-4 py-2 text-sm bg-gray-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-300"
>
{showCollaboration ? 'Hide Collaboration' : 'Show Collaboration'}
{/* Errors */}
{errors.length > 0 && (
Processing Errors:
{errors.map((error, index) => (
-
• {error}
))}
{fallbackUsed && (
⚠️ Fallback to Gemini API was used
The system automatically switched to Gemini API after Ollama encountered issues.
This ensures your request was processed despite local model problems.
)}
)}
{/* Response */}
{response && (
Analysis Result
Generated by {fallbackUsed ? 'Gemini (fallback)' : activeModel}
{response}
)}
{/* Right Panel */}
{showCollaboration && agentDecisions.length > 0 && (
agentDecisions={agentDecisions}
activeModel={activeModel}
/>
)}
{/* Agent Communication Log */}
{agentDecisions.length > 0 && (
Agent Communication
{agentDecisions.map((agent) => (
{agent.agent}
Confidence: {Math.round(agent.confidence * 100)}%
{agent.decision.substring(0, 80)}...
))}
)}
);
};
export default App;
5. Testing Suite for Ollama Integration

// tests/ollamaBridge.test.ts
import { OllamaBridge, AgentResponseParser } from '../services/nexusEngine';
import axios from 'axios';
import MockAdapter from 'axios-mock-adapter';
describe('OllamaBridge', () => {
let bridge: OllamaBridge;
let mock: MockAdapter;
beforeAll(() => {
bridge = OllamaBridge.getInstance();
mock = new MockAdapter(axios);
});
afterEach(() => {
mock.reset();
});
afterAll(() => {
mock.restore();
});
describe('queryOllama', () => {
it('should successfully query Ollama with valid response', async () => {
const mockResponse = {
model: 'llama3',
response: 'Test response from Ollama',
done: true,
context: [1, 2, 3]
};
mock.onPost('http://localhost:11434/api/generate').reply(200, mockResponse);
const result = await bridge.queryOllama('Test prompt', 'llama3');
expect(result.model).toBe('llama3');
expect(result.response).toBe('Test response from Ollama');
expect(result.done).toBe(true);
});
it('should handle connection refused error', async () => {
mock.onPost('http://localhost:11434/api/generate').networkError();
await expect(bridge.queryOllama('Test prompt')).rejects.toThrow(/Cannot connect to Ollama/);
});
it('should handle timeout error', async () => {
mock.onPost('http://localhost:11434/api/generate').timeout();
await expect(bridge.queryOllama('Test prompt')).rejects.toThrow(/timed out/);
});
it('should handle model not found error', async () => {
mock.onPost('http://localhost:11434/api/generate').reply(404, {
error: 'model not found'
});
await expect(bridge.queryOllama('Test prompt', 'nonexistent-model'))
.rejects.toThrow(/Model not found/);
});
it('should handle rate limiting', async () => {
mock.onPost('http://localhost:11434/api/generate').reply(429);
await expect(bridge.queryOllama('Test prompt')).rejects.toThrow(/rate limit exceeded/);
});
});
describe('fallback mechanism', () => {
beforeEach(() => {
process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY = 'test-key';
});
afterEach(() => {
delete process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY;
});
it('should fallback to Gemini when Ollama fails', async () => {
mock.onPost('http://localhost:11434/api/generate').reply(500);
mock.onPost('https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-pro:generateContent')
.reply(200, {
candidates: [{
content: {
parts: [{ text: 'Fallback response from Gemini' }]
}
}]
});
// This should not throw because fallback should succeed
const result = await bridge.queryOllama('Test prompt');
expect(result.model).toContain('gemini-pro');
expect(result.response).toBe('Fallback response from Gemini');
});
});
describe('model discovery', () => {
it('should discover available models', async () => {
const mockModels = {
models: [
{ name: 'llama3', modified_at: '2024-01-01' },
{ name: 'mistral', modified_at: '2024-01-01' }
]
};
mock.onGet('http://localhost:11434/api/tags').reply(200, mockModels);
const models = await bridge.discoverModels(true);
expect(models).toHaveLength(2);
expect(models).toContain('llama3');
expect(models).toContain('mistral');
});
it('should cache model discovery', async () => {
const mockModels = {
models: [{ name: 'llama3', modified_at: '2024-01-01' }]
};
mock.onGet('http://localhost:11434/api/tags').reply(200, mockModels);
// First call
const models1 = await bridge.discoverModels();
expect(mock.history.get.length).toBe(1);
// Second call should use cache
const models2 = await bridge.discoverModels();
expect(mock.history.get.length).toBe(1);
// Force refresh
const models3 = await bridge.discoverModels(true);
expect(mock.history.get.length).toBe(2);
});
});
});
describe('AgentResponseParser', () => {
describe('parseAgentResponse', () => {
it('should parse structured JSON response', () => {
const rawResponse = `{
"agent": "CODE",
"decision": "Implement using Python",
"reasoning": "Python is suitable for this task",
"confidence": 0.8
}`;
const parsed = AgentResponseParser.parseAgentResponse(rawResponse, 'CODE');
expect(parsed.agent).toBe('CODE');
expect(parsed.decision).toBe('Implement using Python');
expect(parsed.reasoning).toBe('Python is suitable for this task');
expect(parsed.confidence).toBe(0.8);
});
it('should parse markdown with headers', () => {
const rawResponse = `# Agent Analysis
## Decision
Use React for the frontend
## Reasoning
React provides the necessary components and ecosystem
## Confidence
0.9`;
const parsed = AgentResponseParser.parseAgentResponse(rawResponse, 'CUBE');
expect(parsed.decision).toBe('Use React for the frontend');
expect(parsed.reasoning).toBe('React provides the necessary components and ecosystem');
});
it('should parse key-value format', () => {
const rawResponse = `Agent: WORK
Decision: Implement in two phases
Reasoning: This allows for iterative testing
Confidence: 75%`;
const parsed = AgentResponseParser.parseAgentResponse(rawResponse, 'WORK');
expect(parsed.agent).toBe('WORK');
expect(parsed.decision).toBe('Implement in two phases');
expect(parsed.confidence).toBeGreaterThan(0.7);
});
it('should parse natural language as fallback', () => {
const rawResponse = `I think we should use TypeScript. It provides better type safety and developer experience. The confidence is about 70%.`;
const parsed = AgentResponseParser.parseAgentResponse(rawResponse, 'CODE');
expect(parsed.decision).toContain('TypeScript');
expect(parsed.reasoning).toBeTruthy();
});
it('should create fallback response when parsing completely fails', () => {
const rawResponse = `{ invalid json`;
const parsed = AgentResponseParser.parseAgentResponse(rawResponse, 'TEST');
expect(parsed.agent).toBe('TEST');
expect(parsed.decision).toBe('Unable to parse specific decision');
expect(parsed.confidence).toBeLessThan(0.2);
});
});
describe('confidence extraction', () => {
it('should extract confidence from percentage', () => {
const text = `I am 85% confident about this decision.`;
const confidence = AgentResponseParser['extractConfidenceFromText'](text);
expect(confidence).toBe(0.85);
});
it('should extract confidence from decimal', () => {
const text = `Confidence: 0.75`;
const confidence = AgentResponseParser['extractConfidenceFromText'](text);
expect(confidence).toBe(0.75);
});
it('should use default confidence when not found', () => {
const text = `This is a decision without explicit confidence.`;
const confidence = AgentResponseParser['extractConfidenceFromText'](text);
expect(confidence).toBe(0.5);
});
});
});
// Integration test for minimized Ollama version
describe('Minimized Ollama Integration', () => {
let bridge: OllamaBridge;
beforeAll(() => {
bridge = OllamaBridge.getInstance();
});
it('should handle non-standard response formats', async () => {
// Simulate minimized Ollama response
const mockResponses = [
// Format 1: Simple string response (non-JSON)
'This is a direct response\n',
// Format 2: Incomplete JSON
'{"response": "Partial',
// Format 3: Extra whitespace and newlines
'\n\n{"model": "test", "response": "Test", "done": true}\n\n',
// Format 4: Missing fields
'{"text": "Alternative field name"}',
];
for (const mockResponse of mockResponses) {
// Test that parser doesn't crash on any format
const parsed = AgentResponseParser.parseAgentResponse(mockResponse, 'TEST');
expect(parsed).toHaveProperty('agent', 'TEST');
expect(parsed).toHaveProperty('decision');
expect(parsed).toHaveProperty('reasoning');
expect(parsed.confidence).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.1);
}
});
it('should handle streaming responses', async () => {
const streamResponse = [
'{"response": "Chunk", "done": false}\n',
'{"response": "1", "done": false}\n',
'{"response": " of data", "done": false}\n',
'{"response": "", "done": true, "context": [1,2,3]}\n'
].join('');
// Test streaming parsing
const result = bridge['parseStreamResponse'](streamResponse, 'test-model');
expect(result.response).toBe('Chunk1 of data');
expect(result.done).toBe(true);
expect(result.context).toEqual([1, 2, 3]);
});
it('should test basic Ollama connectivity', async () => {
const result = await bridge.testConnection();
// Test should either succeed or fail gracefully
expect(result).toHaveProperty('success');
expect(result).toHaveProperty('message');
if (!result.success) {
expect(result.message).toContain('Connection test failed');
}
});
});
6. Environment Configuration
typescript
// config/index.ts
export const config = {
ollamaHost: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_OLLAMA_HOST || 'http://localhost:11434',
ollamaTimeout: parseInt(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_OLLAMA_TIMEOUT || '30000'),
ollamaRetryAttempts: parseInt(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_OLLAMA_RETRIES || '2'),
geminiApiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GEMINI_API_KEY || '',
enableFallback: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_FALLBACK !== 'false',
enableStreaming: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_STREAMING === 'true',
// Agent configuration
defaultAgents: ['CUBE', 'CORE', 'LOOP', 'WAVE', 'SIGN', 'LINE', 'COIN', 'WORK'],
agentTemperature: {
CUBE: 0.3,
CORE: 0.3,
LOOP: 0.5,
WAVE: 0.7,
SIGN: 0.8,
LINE: 0.6,
COIN: 0.4,

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# startup.sh - Enhanced startup for NEXUS AI System
set -e
echo "🚀 Starting NEXUS AI Orchestration System v5.0"
echo "=============================================="
# Check for required dependencies
check_dependency() {
if ! command -v "$1" &> /dev/null; then
echo "❌ Missing dependency: $1"
echo " Please install $1 before continuing."
return 1
fi
echo "✅ $1 found"
}
echo -e "\n🔍 Checking dependencies..."
check_dependency "node"
check_dependency "npm"
check_dependency "ollama" || {
echo -e "\n⚠ Ollama not found. Installing..."
curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh
}
# Check if Ollama is running
echo -e "\n🔌 Checking Ollama service..."
if ! curl -s http://localhost:11434/api/tags >/dev/null 2>&1; then
echo "🔄 Starting Ollama service..."
ollama serve > /dev/null 2>&1 &
OLLAMA_PID=$!
sleep 5
# Verify Ollama started
if curl -s http://localhost:11434/api/tags >/dev/null 2>&1; then
echo "✅ Ollama service started (PID: $OLLAMA_PID)"
else
echo "❌ Failed to start Ollama service"
exit 1
fi
else
echo "✅ Ollama service is already running"
fi
# Check for models
echo -e "\n🤖 Checking Ollama models..."
MODELS=$(curl -s http://localhost:11434/api/tags | jq -r '.models[].name' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
if [[ -z "$MODELS" ]]; then
echo "⚠ No models found. Pulling llama3..."
ollama pull llama3
else
echo "✅ Found models:"
echo "$MODELS" | while read -r model; do
echo " • $model"
done
fi
# Setup environment
echo -e "\n⚙️ Setting up environment..."
if [[ ! -f .env ]]; then
cat > .env << EOF
# Ollama Configuration
OLLAMA_HOST=http://localhost:11434
OLLAMA_MODEL=llama3
OLLAMA_TIMEOUT=30000
OLLAMA_RETRIES=2
# Fallback Configuration (optional)
# GEMINI_API_KEY=your_gemini_api_key_here
# Agent Configuration
ENABLE_MULTI_AGENT=true
ENABLE_AGENT_COMMUNICATION=true
ENABLE_FALLBACK=true
ENABLE_STREAMING=false
EOF
echo "✅ Created .env file"
else
echo "✅ .env file already exists"
fi
# Install npm dependencies
echo -e "\n📦 Installing npm dependencies..."
npm install
# Initialize database
echo -e "\n🗄️ Initializing database..."
node -e "
const { execSync } = require('child_process');
try {
execSync('mkdir -p ~/_/ai/.db', { stdio: 'inherit' });
console.log('Database directory created');
} catch (error) {
console.error('Failed to create database directory:', error);
}
"
# Build the application
echo -e "\n🔨 Building application..."
npm run build
# Start the application
echo -e "\n🎯 Starting NEXUS AI System..."
echo "=============================================="
echo "🌐 Web Interface: http://localhost:3000"
echo "🤖 API Endpoint: http://localhost:3000/api"
echo "🔧 Ollama: http://localhost:11434"
echo "📊 Database: ~/_/ai/.db/ai_memory.db"
echo "📝 Logs: ~/_/ai/.db/ai_system.log"
echo "=============================================="
echo -e "\n📋 Available commands:"
echo " ./orchestrator.sh analyze \"Your prompt\""
echo " ./orchestrator.sh models"
echo " ./orchestrator.sh test"
echo " ./orchestrator.sh chat \"Your message\""
echo -e "\n🚀 Starting server..."
# Start the application
npm start

PHASE 3 — WEBSOCKET STREAM ROUTER
=====================================================
Write server.js:
import WebSocket, { WebSocketServer } from "ws";
export const timeline = [];
const wss = new WebSocketServer({ port: 2244 });
let clients = [];
wss.on("connection", ws => {
clients.push(ws);
ws.on("close", () => clients = clients.filter(c => c !== ws));
});
export function broadcast(agent, token, sha, genesis, md5) {
const msg = JSON.stringify({ agent, token, sha, genesis, md5, time: Date.now() });
timeline.push(msg);
clients.forEach(c => c.send(msg));
}

PHASE 4 — TIME-TRAVEL ENGINE
=====================================================
Write timeline.js:
import fs from "fs";
export function saveState(md5, data) {
fs.appendFileSync("memory/timeline.db", JSON.stringify({ md5, data }) + "\n");
}
export function replay(md5) {
return fs.readFileSync("memory/timeline.db","utf8")
.split("\n")
.filter(l => l.includes(md5));
}
=====================================================
PHASE 5 — VECTOR MEMORY ENGINE
=====================================================
Write vector.js:
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
import sqlite3, hashlib, sys
model = SentenceTransformer('all-MiniLM-L6-v2')
conn = sqlite3.connect("memory/vectors.db")
c = conn.cursor()
c.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory (md5 TEXT, sha TEXT, vector BLOB, text TEXT)")
text = sys.stdin.read()
vec = model.encode(text).tobytes()
md5 = hashlib.md5(text.encode()).hexdigest()
sha = hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()
c.execute("INSERT INTO memory VALUES (?,?,?,?)", (md5, sha, vec, text))
conn.commit()

PHASE 6 — NEXUS ORCHESTRATOR
=====================================================
Write ai.sh:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
mkdir -p entropy memory
GENESIS=$(date +%s%N | sha256sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
PROMPT="$*"
echo "{ \"genesis\": \"$GENESIS\" }" > memory/memory.json
declare -A AGENTS
AGENTS[coder]="deepseek-coder"
AGENTS[agi]="deepseek-r1"
AGENTS[nemodian]="wave"
AGENTS[sysop]="llama3.1"
stream_agent() {
name=$1
model=$2
ollama run "$model" "$PROMPT" --stream | while read -r token; do
echo "$token" >> entropy/$name.stream
sha=$(echo -n "$token" | sha256sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
md5=$(echo -n "$sha" | md5sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
node -e "
import {broadcast} from './server.js';
broadcast('$name','$token','$sha','$GENESIS','$md5');
"
done
cat entropy/$name.stream | python3 vector.js
}
for a in "${!AGENTS[@]}"; do
stream_agent "$a" "${AGENTS[$a]}" &
done
wait

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
mkdir -p entropy memory
GENESIS=$(date +%s%N | sha256sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
PROMPT="$*"
echo "{ \"genesis\": \"$GENESIS\", \"states\": {} }" > memory/memory.json
declare -A AGENTS
AGENTS[coder]="deepseek-coder"
AGENTS[agi]="deepseek-r1"
AGENTS[nemodian]="wave"
AGENTS[sysop]="llama3.1"
stream_agent() {
name=$1
model=$2
ollama run "$model" "$PROMPT" --stream | while read -r token; do
echo "$token" >> "entropy/$name.stream"
sha=$(echo -n "$token" | sha256sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
echo "$sha" >> "entropy/$name.sha"
node -e "
import {broadcast} from './server.js';
broadcast('$name', '$token', '$sha', '$GENESIS');
"
done
}
for a in "${!AGENTS[@]}"; do
stream_agent "$a" "${AGENTS[$a]}" &
done
wait

}\n\nmkdir -p \"$AI_MEMORY_DIR\"\n\n# ------------------------------\n# Initialize AI environment\n# ------------------------------\nai_init() {\n log_info \"Initializing AI environment...\"\n mkdir -p \"$AI_MEMORY_DIR\"\n log_success \"Environment ready\"\n}\n\n# ------------------------------\n# Start Ollama server\n# ------------------------------\nai_start() {\n log_info \"Starting AI server (Ollama)...\"\n if command -v ollama >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n while IFS= read -r -d '' topic; do\n known_topics+=(\"$(basename \"$topic\")\")\n done < <(find \"$AI_MEMORY_DIR\" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d -print0 2>/dev/null)\n fi\n\n # --- Auto-tagging ---\n local prompt_words\n read -r -a prompt_words <<< \"$(echo \"$prompt\" | tr -cs '[:alnum:]' ' ')\"\n local matched_topics=()\n local unique_words=()\n for word in \"${prompt_words[@]}\"; do\n word=\"${word,,}\"\n [[ ${#word} -gt 2 ]] && unique_words+=(\"$word\")\n done\n unique_words=($(printf \"%s\\n\" \"${unique_words[@]}\" | sort -u))\n\n for word in \"${unique_words[@]}\"; do\n for topic in \"${known_topics[@]}\"; do\n if [[ \"${topic,,}\" == *\"$word\"* || \"$word\" == *\"${topic,,}\"* ]]; then\n matched_topics+=(\"$topic\")\n fi\n done\n done\n\n [[ ${#matched_topics[@]} -eq 0 ]] && matched_topics=(\"general\")\n matched_topics=($(printf \"%s\\n\" \"${matched_topics[@]}\" | sort -u))\n\n # --- Load memory ---\n local context=\"\"\n if [[ \"$use_memory\" == \"true\" ]]; then\n for topic in \"${matched_topics[@]}\"; do\n local topic_dir=\"$AI_MEMORY_DIR/$topic\"\n if [[ -f \"$topic_dir/summary.mem\" ]]; then\n context+=\"=== $topic ===$(cat \"$topic_dir/summary.mem\")\"$'\\n\\n'\n fi\n done\n fi\n\n local full_prompt=\"${context}${prompt}\"\n log_info \"Thinking (memory=${use_memory}, delay=${delay}s, topics=${matched_topics[*]})...\"\n\n # --- API call ---\n local temp_file\n temp_file=$(mktemp)\n local http_code\n http_code=$(curl -s -w \"%{http_code}\" -X POST \"$OLLAMA_HOST/api/generate\" \\\n -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n -d \"{\\\"model\\\":\\\"$OLLAMA_MODEL\\\",\\\"prompt\\\":\\\"$full_prompt\\\",\\\"stream\\\":true}\" \\\n -o \"$temp_file\")\n\n if [[ $http_code -ne 200 ]]; then\n log_error \"API request failed with HTTP $http_code\"\n [[ -f \"$temp_file\" ]] && log_error \"Error: $(cat \"$temp_file\")\"\n rm -f \"$temp_file\"\n return 1\n fi\n\n # --- Process tokens ---\n local token_count=0\n local response=\"\"\n while IFS= read -r line; 🧠 Phase 1 — NEXUS Entropy Router
Right now you route by string similarity:

🧬 Step 1 — Build a word entropy table
Add this to your script (top-level):
ENTROPY_INDEX="$AI_MEMORY_DIR/.entropy.index"
Now create this function:
entropy_update() {
local text="$1"
mkdir -p "$AI_MEMORY_DIR"
echo "$text" | tr -cs '[:alnum:]' '\n' | tr 'A-Z' 'a-z' |
awk 'length($0)>2 {count[$0]++} END {for (w in count) print w, count[w]}' |
while read -r word count; do
grep -q "^$word " "$ENTROPY_INDEX" 2>/dev/null \
&& sed -i "s/^$word .*/$word $((count + $(grep "^$word " "$ENTROPY_INDEX" | awk '{print $2}')))/" "$ENTROPY_INDEX" \
|| echo "$word $count" >> "$ENTROPY_INDEX"
done
}
Call it every time memory is saved:
Inside:
echo -e "PROMPT: $prompt\nRESPONSE: $response" > "$memory_file"
Add:
entropy_update "$prompt $response"
Now the AI builds a global word frequency field — its universe of meaning.
🧬 Step 2 — Replace topic matching with entropy-weighted routing
Remove this weak block:
for word in "${unique_words[@]}"; do
for topic in "${known_topics[@]}"; do
...
Replace with:
declare -A topic_score
for word in "${unique_words[@]}"; do
freq=$(grep "^$word " "$ENTROPY_INDEX" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $2}')
[[ -z "$freq" ]] && continue
entropy=$((100000 / freq)) # rare = high entropy
for topic in "${known_topics[@]}"; do
if grep -iq "$word" "$AI_MEMORY_DIR/$topic"/*.mem 2>/dev/null; then
topic_score[$topic]=$(( ${topic_score[$topic]:-0} + entropy ))
fi
done
done
Then select dominant topics:
matched_topics=($(for t in "${!topic_score[@]}"; do
echo "${topic_score[$t]} $t"
done | sort -nr | head -n 3 | awk '{print $2}'))
[[ ${#matched_topics[@]} -eq 0 ]] && matched_topics=("general")
You now have entropy-based recall.
The AI chooses memory by semantic gravity, not word coincidence.

ai_swarm() {
local prompt="$1"
local context="$2"
local tmpdir
tmpdir=$(mktemp -d)
for agent in "${AGENTS[@]}"; do
(

--arg m "$OLLAMA_MODEL" \
'{model:$m,prompt:$p,stream:false}')
curl -s "$OLLAMA_HOST/api/generate" \
-H "Content-Type: application/json" \
-d "$json" | jq -r '.response' > "$tmpdir/$agent.txt"
) &
done
wait
cat "$tmpdir"/*.txt > "$tmpdir/swarm.all"
echo "$tmpdir"
}

🔄 2. Watchdog (Auto-resurrection)
Create a new file:
~/bin/nexus-watchdog.sh
Put this in it:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
AI="$HOME/bin/ai"
HB="$HOME/.ai_memory/.heartbeat"
while true; do
sleep 10
if [[ ! -f "$HB" ]]; then
"$AI" start
continue
fi
now=$(date +%s)
last=$(cat "$HB")
delta=$((now-last))
if (( delta > 30 )); then
echo "[WATCHDOG] NEXUS stalled — restarting"
pkill ollama
"$AI" start
fi
done
Make it executable:
chmod +x ~/bin/nexus-watchdog.sh
Add to .bashrc:
nohup ~/bin/nexus-watchdog.sh >/dev/null 2>&1 &
Now NEXUS survives crashes, freezes, and restarts.

🧬 1. The Will Engine
Add:
WILL="$AI_MEMORY_DIR/.will"
touch "$WILL"
Add:
will_next() {
goals=$(ls -1t "$GOAL_DIR"/*.goal 2>/dev/null | head -n 3 | xargs cat)
self=$(ls -1t "$SELF_DIR"/*.self 2>/dev/null | head -n 1 | xargs cat)
prompt="
You are NEXUS.
SELF:
$self
GOALS:
$goals
What is the single most important thing you should do next?
Return:
ACTION:
"
json=$(jq -n --arg p "$prompt" --arg m "$OLLAMA_MODEL" \
'{model:$m,prompt:$p,stream:false}')
action=$(curl -s "$OLLAMA_HOST/api/generate" \
-H "Content-Type: application/json" \
-d "$json" | jq -r '.response')
echo "$action" > "$WILL"
echo "$action"
}
🧬 2. The Action Loop
Add:
nexus_loop() {
while true; do
heartbeat
action=$(will_next)
echo "[NEXUS] $action"
# execute only safe actions for now
if echo "$action" | grep -iq "analyze\|think\|reflect\|summarize"; then
ai_process_dynamic "$action"
fi
self_reflect >/dev/null
goal_form >/dev/null
soul_write
sleep 30
done
}
🧬 3. Start it at boot
Add to .bashrc:
nohup $HOME/bin/ai nexus_loop >/dev/null 2>&1 &

bash
#!/bin/bash
# NEXUS-2244 Construction Script
set -e
echo "🚀 Initiating NEXUS-2244 Cognitive Kernel Construction"
echo "====================================================="
# Fix existing WSL AI system errors first
echo "🔧 Fixing existing WSL AI system errors..."
cd ~/_/ai
# Fix the jq command syntax errors
cat > fix_ai_errors.sh << 'FIX'
#!/bin/bash
# Fix jq syntax errors in ai.sh
sed -i 's/jq -nc --arg txt "$txt" --arg model "$EMBED_MODEL" \{model:\$model, input:\$txt\}/jq -nc --arg txt "$txt" --arg model "$EMBED_MODEL" '\''{model:\$model, input:\$txt}'\''/' ai.sh
sed -i "s/jq -nc --arg model \"\$OLLAMA_MODEL\" --arg prompt \"\$ollama_prompt\" \{model:\$model, prompt:\$prompt, stream:false, options:{temperature:\$temperature, num_predict:\$num_predict\}\}/jq -nc --arg model \"\$OLLAMA_MODEL\" --arg prompt \"\$ollama_prompt\" --argjson temperature \"\$temperature\" --argjson num_predict 256 '{model:\$model, prompt:\$prompt, stream:false, options:{temperature:\$temperature, num_predict:\$num_predict}}'/" ai.sh
# Fix Python embedding function
cat > fix_embed.py << 'PYFIX'
import sys, base64, struct, math
def decode(b64):
data = base64.b64decode(b64)
return struct.unpack("<%df" % (len(data)//4), data)
if __name__ == "__main__":
if len(sys.argv) < 3:
sys.exit(1)
a = decode(sys.argv[1])
b = decode(sys.argv[2])
dot = sum(x*y for x,y in zip(a,b))
norm_a = math.sqrt(sum(x*x for x in a))
norm_b = math.sqrt(sum(y*y for y in b))
result = dot/(norm_a*norm_b) if norm_a and norm_b else 0.0
print(result)
PYFIX
# Replace the embedded Python in bash script
sed -i "s/python3 - <<'PY' \"\$@\"$/python3 fix_embed.py \"\$@\"/" ai.sh
FIX
chmod +x fix_ai_errors.sh
./fix_ai_errors.sh
rm fix_ai_errors.sh fix_embed.py
echo "✅ Fixed syntax errors in WSL AI system"
# Now construct NEXUS-2244
echo ""
echo "🏗️ Building NEXUS-2244 Cognitive Kernel"
echo "====================================================="
# Create base directory
cd ~
rm -rf nexus2244
mkdir -p nexus2244
cd nexus2244
echo "📁 Creating directory structure..."
mkdir -p entropy memory ui
echo "📝 Writing Docker configuration..."
# Dockerfile
cat > Dockerfile << 'DOCKER'
FROM ubuntu:24.04
ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
# Install system dependencies
RUN apt update && apt install -y \
curl \
git \
jq \
nodejs \
npm \
python3 \
python3-pip \
ca-certificates \
sqlite3 \
wget \
&& rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
# Install Ollama
RUN curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh
# Install Python dependencies
RUN pip3 install sentence-transformers numpy
WORKDIR /nexus
# Copy application files
COPY . .
# Install Node.js dependencies
RUN npm install ws sqlite3
EXPOSE 2244
CMD ["bash", "ai.sh"]
DOCKER

app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json());
// Initialize databases
const timelineDb = new sqlite3.Database('memory/timeline.db');
const vectorsDb = new sqlite3.Database('memory/vectors.db');
const goalsDb = new sqlite3.Database('memory/goals.db');
// Promisify database operations

const dbAll = promisify(timelineDb.all.bind(timelineDb));

// Agent definitions
const AGENTS = {
coder: { model: 'deepseek-coder', role: 'Code generation and implementation' },
agi: { model: 'deepseek-r1', role: 'General reasoning and problem solving' },
nemodian: { model: 'wave', role: 'Wave-like pattern recognition' },

// WebSocket server for real-time streaming
const wss = new WebSocketServer({ port: 2244 });
let clients = [];
let timeline = [];
// Meta-agents for swarm intelligence
const META_AGENTS = {
LOGOS: { role: 'Measures consistency and coherence' },
CHAOS: { role: 'Measures novelty and divergence' },
NOMOS: { role: 'Measures goal alignment' }
};
// Hash utilities
function generateHash(text) {
const crypto = require('crypto');
return {
md5: crypto.createHash('md5').update(text).digest('hex'),
sha256: crypto.createHash('sha256').update(text).digest('hex'),
genesis: crypto.createHash('sha256')
.update(Date.now().toString() + text)
.digest('hex')
};
}
// Calculate entropy (simple token-based)
function calculateEntropy(tokens) {
if (!tokens || tokens.length === 0) return 0.0;
const tokenCounts = {};
tokens.forEach(token => {
tokenCounts[token] = (tokenCounts[token] || 0) + 1;
});
const total = tokens.length;
let entropy = 0;
Object.values(tokenCounts).forEach(count => {
const probability = count / total;
entropy -= probability * Math.log2(probability);
});
return entropy;
}
// WebSocket connection handler
wss.on('connection', (ws) => {
clients.push(ws);
console.log(`New WebSocket connection. Total clients: ${clients.length}`);
// Send current timeline
ws.send(JSON.stringify({
type: 'timeline_init',
data: timeline.slice(-100) // Last 100 entries
}));
ws.on('close', () => {
clients = clients.filter(c => c !== ws);
console.log(`WebSocket closed. Remaining clients: ${clients.length}`);
});
ws.on('error', (error) => {
console.error('WebSocket error:', error);
});
});
// Broadcast function for real-time updates
function broadcast(agent, token, hashes, entropy = 0.0) {
const message = {
type: 'token',
agent,
token,
...hashes,
entropy,
timestamp: Date.now(),
parent: timeline.length > 0 ? timeline[timeline.length - 1].md5 : null
};
timeline.push(message);
// Store in database
dbRun(
`INSERT INTO timeline (md5, sha256, agent, token, genesis, entropy, parent_hash)
VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
[hashes.md5, hashes.sha256, agent, token, hashes.genesis, entropy, message.parent]
).catch(err => console.error('Database error:', err));
// Broadcast to all connected clients
clients.forEach(client => {
if (client.readyState === 1) { // OPEN state
client.send(JSON.stringify(message));
}
});
}
// Goal management
async function createGoal(description, priority = 1) {
const hash = generateHash(description).md5;
await dbRun(
`INSERT INTO goals (description, priority, hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?)`,
[description, priority, hash]
);
return hash;
}
async function scoreGoal(goalId, score) {
await dbRun(
`UPDATE goals SET success_score = ?, last_updated = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?`,
[score, goalId]
);
}
async function getActiveGoals() {
return await dbAll(
`SELECT * FROM goals WHERE status = 'active' ORDER BY priority DESC, success_score DESC`
);
}
// HTTP API endpoints
app.post('/api/ask', async (req, res) => {
try {
const { prompt, agent, context = [], stream = false } = req.body;
if (!prompt) {
return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Prompt is required' });
}
const targetAgent = agent || 'sysop';
const agentConfig = AGENTS[targetAgent];
if (!agentConfig) {
return res.status(400).json({ error: `Unknown agent: ${targetAgent}` });
}
// Generate hashes
const hashes = generateHash(prompt);
// Store in memory
const memoryEntry = {
prompt,
hashes,
agent: targetAgent,
timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
};
fs.appendFileSync(
'memory/memory.json',
JSON.stringify(memoryEntry) + '\n'
);
// If streaming, setup WebSocket response
if (stream) {
res.json({
status: 'streaming',
genesis: hashes.genesis,
agent: targetAgent,
streamUrl: `ws://localhost:2244`
});
} else {
// Direct Ollama query (non-streaming)
const ollamaResponse = await queryOllama(prompt, agentConfig.model);
res.json({
status: 'success',
response: ollamaResponse,
agent: targetAgent,
model: agentConfig.model,
...hashes
});
}
} catch (error) {
console.error('API error:', error);
res.status(500).json({ error: error.message });
}
});
app.get('/api/models', async (req, res) => {
try {
const response = await axios.get('http://localhost:11434/api/tags');
const models = response.data.models || [];
res.json({
status: 'success',
models: models.map(m => ({
name: m.name,
digest: m.digest,
details: {
size: m.size,
modified_at: m.modified_at,
family: m.name.split(':')[0]
}
}))
});
} catch (error) {
res.status(500).json({
status: 'error',
message: 'Ollama not available',
details: error.message
});
}
});
app.get('/api/timeline', async (req, res) => {
try {
const limit = parseInt(req.query.limit) || 100;
const entries = await dbAll(
`SELECT * FROM timeline ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?`,
[limit]
);
res.json({
status: 'success',
count: entries.length,
timeline: entries
});
} catch (error) {
res.status(500).json({ error: error.message });
}
});
app.post('/api/goals', async (req, res) => {
try {
const { description, priority } = req.body;
const goalHash = await createGoal(description, priority || 1);
res.json({
status: 'success',
goalHash,
message: 'Goal created successfully'
});
} catch (error) {
res.status(500).json({ error: error.message });
}
});
// Ollama query function with fallback
async function queryOllama(prompt, model, options = {}) {
const defaultOptions = {
temperature: 0.7,
num_predict: 512,
top_p: 0.9,
repeat_penalty: 1.1,
stop: ["", "\n\n"]
};
const requestOptions = { ...defaultOptions, ...options };
try {
const response = await axios.post('http://localhost:11434/api/generate', {
model,
prompt,
stream: false,
options: requestOptions
}, {
timeout: 30000
});
return response.data.response;
} catch (error) {
console.error('Ollama query failed:', error.message);
// Fallback logic
if (process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY) {
console.log('Falling back to Gemini API');
return await queryGemini(prompt);
}
throw new Error(`Ollama query failed: ${error.message}`);
}
}
// Gemini fallback (placeholder)
async function queryGemini(prompt) {
// This would integrate with Gemini API
return `[Gemini Fallback] Response to: ${prompt.substring(0, 100)}...`;
}
// Start HTTP server
const PORT = 7070;
app.listen(PORT, () => {
console.log(`NEXUS HTTP API listening on port ${PORT}`);
console.log(`WebSocket server listening on port 2244`);
console.log('NEXUS-2244 Cognitive Kernel initialized');
});

echo "⏱️ Creating Time-Travel Engine..."
# timeline.js
cat > timeline.js << 'TIMELINE'
import fs from 'fs';
import crypto from 'crypto';
export class TimelineEngine {
constructor(dbPath = 'memory/timeline.db') {
this.dbPath = dbPath;
this.ensureDatabase();
}
ensureDatabase() {
if (!fs.existsSync(this.dbPath)) {
fs.writeFileSync(this.dbPath, '');
}
}
saveState(md5, data) {
const entry = {
md5,
data,
timestamp: Date.now(),
hash: crypto.createHash('sha256')
.update(JSON.stringify(data))
.digest('hex')
};
fs.appendFileSync(
this.dbPath,
JSON.stringify(entry) + '\n'
);
return entry.hash;
}
replay(md5) {
if (!fs.existsSync(this.dbPath)) {
return [];
}
const content = fs.readFileSync(this.dbPath, 'utf8');
return content
.split('\n')
.filter(line => line.trim())
.map(line => JSON.parse(line))
.filter(entry => entry.md5 === md5);
}
getBranch(md5) {
if (!fs.existsSync(this.dbPath)) {
return [];
}
const content = fs.readFileSync(this.dbPath, 'utf8');
const entries = content
.split('\n')
.filter(line => line.trim())
.map(line => JSON.parse(line));
// Build tree structure
const tree = [];
const visited = new Set();
function buildTree(currentMd5, depth = 0) {
if (visited.has(currentMd5) || depth > 20) return [];
visited.add(currentMd5);
const children = entries.filter(e => e.data?.parent === currentMd5);
return children.map(child => ({
...child,
children: buildTree(child.md5, depth + 1)
}));
}
return buildTree(md5);
}
calculateEntropy(md5) {
const entries = this.replay(md5);
if (entries.length === 0) return 0.0;
// Calculate token diversity
const allText = entries.map(e => e.data?.text || '').join(' ');
const tokens = allText.split(/\s+/).filter(t => t.length > 0);
if (tokens.length === 0) return 0.0;
const tokenCounts = {};
tokens.forEach(token => {
tokenCounts[token] = (tokenCounts[token] || 0) + 1;
});
const total = tokens.length;
let entropy = 0;
Object.values(tokenCounts).forEach(count => {
const probability = count / total;
entropy -= probability * Math.log2(probability);
});
return entropy;
}
}

echo "🧠 Creating Vector Memory Engine..."
# vector.js
cat > vector.js << 'VECTOR'
#!/usr/bin/env node
import { SentenceTransformer } from 'sentence-transformers';
import sqlite3 from 'sqlite3';
import { open } from 'sqlite3';
import crypto from 'crypto';
import fs from 'fs';
async function main() {
// Read text from stdin
let text = '';
process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');
for await (const chunk of process.stdin) {
text += chunk;
}
if (!text.trim()) {
console.error('No text provided to vectorize');
process.exit(1);
}
// Generate hashes
const md5 = crypto.createHash('md5').update(text).digest('hex');
const sha256 = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(text).digest('hex');
try {
// Initialize sentence transformer
const model = new SentenceTransformer('all-MiniLM-L6-v2');
const embedding = await model.encode(text);
// Convert to buffer for storage
const vectorBuffer = Buffer.from(embedding);
// Store in SQLite
const db = await open({
filename: 'memory/vectors.db',
driver: sqlite3.Database
});
await db.run(`
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vectors (
id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
md5 TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
vector BLOB NOT NULL,
text TEXT NOT NULL,
timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
)
`);
await db.run(
`INSERT OR REPLACE INTO vectors (md5, sha256, vector, text) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)`,
[md5, sha256, vectorBuffer, text]
);
await db.close();
// Output result
console.log(JSON.stringify({
status: 'success',
md5,
sha256,
vector_dimensions: embedding.length,
text_preview: text.substring(0, 100) + '...'
}));
} catch (error) {
console.error('Vectorization error:', error);
console.log(JSON.stringify({
status: 'error',
error: error.message,
md5,
sha256
}));
process.exit(1);
}
}
main().catch(console.error);

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
# Colors for output
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m'
# Logging function
log() {
local level="$1"
local message="$2"
local timestamp=$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%3N')
case "$level" in
INFO) echo -e "${GREEN}[INFO]${NC} $message" ;;
WARN) echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARN]${NC} $message" ;;
ERROR) echo -e "${RED}[ERROR]${NC} $message" ;;
DEBUG) echo -e "${BLUE}[DEBUG]${NC} $message" ;;
*) echo "[$level] $message" ;;
esac
}
# Ensure directories exist
mkdir -p entropy memory memory/backups ui
# Generate genesis hash

echo "{ \"genesis\": \"$GENESIS\", \"timestamp\": \"$(date -Iseconds)\", \"prompt\": \"$PROMPT\" }" > memory/memory.json
# Load mind files if they exist
load_mind_files() {

local loaded_content=""
for file in "${mind_files[@]}"; do
if [[ -f "$HOME/ai/$file" ]]; then
log "INFO" "Loading mind file: $file"
loaded_content+="$(cat "$HOME/ai/$file")\n\n"
fi
done
if [[ -n "$loaded_content" ]]; then
echo -e "# PRIOR KNOWLEDGE\n$loaded_content" > entropy/prior_knowledge.txt
fi
}
load_mind_files
# Define agents with enhanced models
declare -A AGENTS
AGENTS[coder]="deepseek-coder"
AGENTS[agi]="deepseek-r1"
AGENTS[nemodian]="wave"
AGENTS[sysop]="llama3.1"
AGENTS[cube]="gemma3:1b"
AGENTS[core]="deepseek-v3.1:671b-cloud"
AGENTS[loop]="loop:latest"
AGENTS[line]="line:latest"
AGENTS[wave]="qwen3-vl:2b"
AGENTS[coin]="stable-code:latest"
AGENTS[code]="phi:2.7b"
AGENTS[work]="deepseek-v3.1:671b-cloud"
# 2π/8 phase rotation
declare -a PHASE_ORDER=(coder agi nemodian sysop cube core loop line wave coin code work)
PHASE_INDEX=$(( $(date +%s) % ${#PHASE_ORDER[@]} ))
# Function to stream agent output
stream_agent() {
local name="$1"
local model="$2"
local phase_offset="$3"
log "INFO" "Starting agent: $name (model: $model, phase: $phase_offset)"
# Create unique stream file
local stream_file="entropy/${name}_${GENESIS}.stream"
local hash_file="entropy/${name}_${GENESIS}.hash"
# Check if model exists
if ! ollama list | grep -q "$model"; then
log "WARN" "Model $model not found, pulling..."
ollama pull "$model" &
fi
# Stream from Ollama
ollama run "$model" "$PROMPT" --stream 2>/dev/null | \
while IFS= read -r token; do
# Skip empty tokens
[[ -z "$token" ]] && continue
# Append to stream file
echo "$token" >> "$stream_file"
# Generate hashes
local sha=$(echo -n "$token" | sha256sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
local md5=$(echo -n "$sha" | md5sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
# Store hash
echo "$sha" >> "$hash_file"
# Broadcast via Node.js
node -e "
import('${PWD}/server.js').then(module => {
const { broadcast, generateHash } = module;
const hashes = { md5: '$md5', sha256: '$sha', genesis: '$GENESIS' };
broadcast('$name', '$token', hashes, $phase_offset);
}).catch(err => console.error('Broadcast error:', err));
" 2>/dev/null &
# Small delay to prevent overwhelming
sleep 0.01
done
# Process completed stream
if [[ -f "$stream_file" ]]; then
log "DEBUG" "Processing $name stream with vector engine"
# Create vector embedding
cat "$stream_file" | node vector.js 2>/dev/null || true
# Calculate entropy
local token_count=$(wc -w < "$stream_file" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
local unique_tokens=$(tr ' ' '\n' < "$stream_file" | sort -u | wc -l 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
local entropy=$(echo "scale=4; $unique_tokens / ($token_count + 1)" | bc 2>/dev/null || echo "0.0")
# Store entropy
echo "$entropy" > "entropy/${name}_${GENESIS}.entropy"
log "INFO" "Agent $name completed: $token_count tokens, entropy: $entropy"
fi
}
# Goal engine functions
initialize_goals() {
log "INFO" "Initializing goal engine..."
node -e "
import('sqlite3').then(sqlite3 => {
const db = new sqlite3.Database('memory/goals.db');
db.run(\`
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS goals (
id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

status TEXT DEFAULT 'active',
created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
last_updated DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
success_score REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
parent_goal INTEGER,
hash TEXT UNIQUE
)
\`);
db.close();
});
" 2>/dev/null &
}
create_goal() {
local description="$1"
local priority="${2:-1}"
node -e "
const crypto = require('crypto');
const db = require('sqlite3').Database('memory/goals.db');
const hash = crypto.createHash('md5').update('$description').digest('hex');
db.run(
'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO goals (description, priority, hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?)',
['$description', $priority, hash],
function(err) {
if (!err) console.log('Goal created:', hash);
db.close();
}
);
" 2>/dev/null
}
# Start goal engine

fi
# Main execution loop

for i in "${!PHASE_ORDER[@]}"; do
agent="${PHASE_ORDER[$i]}"
model="${AGENTS[$agent]}"
if [[ -n "$model" ]]; then
# Calculate phase offset (0.0 to 1.0)
phase_offset=$(echo "scale=2; $i / ${#PHASE_ORDER[@]}" | bc)
# Start agent in background
stream_agent "$agent" "$model" "$phase_offset" &
pids+=($!)
# Limit concurrent agents
if [[ ${#pids[@]} -ge 4 ]]; then
wait -n
pids=($(jobs -p))
fi
fi
done
# Wait for all agents
wait
# Generate summary

);
" 2>/dev/null &
fi
