Perfect ✅ — we can extend the upgrade wrapper to auto-detect all Deepseek / NEXUS AI tasks in your ~/_/ai repo and apply the v4.0 script upgrade to all of them safely in parallel, while keeping backups and logs.
Here’s the fully enhanced auto-upgrade wrapper:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =============================================================================
# AI System Parallel Upgrade – Auto-Upgrade All Deepseek / NEXUS Tasks
# =============================================================================
set -euo pipefail
IFS=$'\n\t'
AI_DIR="${HOME}/_/ai"
SCRIPT_NAME="ai_system.sh"
BACKUP_DIR="${AI_DIR}/.backups"
TIMESTAMP=$(date '+%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
MAX_PARALLEL=4 # Adjust based on system load
mkdir -p "$BACKUP_DIR"
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1️⃣ Detect all Deepseek/NEXUS task directories
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo "[INFO] Scanning for Deepseek/NEXUS tasks..."
TASK_DIRS=($(find "$AI_DIR" -maxdepth 2 -type f -name "$SCRIPT_NAME" -exec dirname {} \;))
if [[ ${#TASK_DIRS[@]} -eq 0 ]]; then
echo "[WARN] No AI tasks found for upgrade."
exit 0
fi
echo "[INFO] Found ${#TASK_DIRS[@]} task(s) to upgrade."
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2️⃣ Function to upgrade a single task
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
upgrade_task() {
local task_dir="$1"
echo "[INFO] Upgrading task at $task_dir"
# Backup existing script
if [[ -f "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME" ]]; then
cp -v "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME" "$BACKUP_DIR/${SCRIPT_NAME}.$(basename "$task_dir").${TIMESTAMP}.bak"
fi
# Backup logs & telemetry
local log_file="$task_dir/.db/ai_system.log"
local telemetry_file="$task_dir/.db/ai_telemetry.jsonl"
[[ -f "$log_file" ]] && cp -v "$log_file" "$BACKUP_DIR/ai_system.log.$(basename "$task_dir").${TIMESTAMP}.bak"
[[ -f "$telemetry_file" ]] && cp -v "$telemetry_file" "$BACKUP_DIR/ai_telemetry.jsonl.$(basename "$task_dir").${TIMESTAMP}.bak"
# Copy updated script
cp -v "./$SCRIPT_NAME" "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME"
chmod +x "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME"
# Optional: dry-run test
"$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME" --dry-run reason "Upgrade test prompt"
echo "[INFO] Upgrade complete for $task_dir"
}
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3️⃣ Export function for parallel execution
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
export -f upgrade_task
export SCRIPT_NAME BACKUP_DIR TIMESTAMP
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4️⃣ Run upgrades in parallel
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo "[INFO] Starting parallel upgrades with $MAX_PARALLEL jobs..."
printf "%s\n" "${TASK_DIRS[@]}" | xargs -n1 -P "$MAX_PARALLEL" -I {} bash -c 'upgrade_task "$@"' _ {}
echo "[INFO] All upgrades completed successfully."

Perfect! 🚀 Let’s upgrade the wrapper so it dynamically throttles concurrency based on your system’s current CPU and memory load—this way, it will never overload your machine while upgrading multiple AI tasks.
Here’s the enhanced dynamic concurrency auto-upgrade wrapper:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =============================================================================
# AI System Parallel Upgrade – Auto-Upgrade All Deepseek / NEXUS Tasks
# with Dynamic Concurrency Throttling
# =============================================================================
set -euo pipefail
IFS=$'\n\t'
AI_DIR="${HOME}/_/ai"
SCRIPT_NAME="ai_system.sh"
BACKUP_DIR="${AI_DIR}/.backups"
TIMESTAMP=$(date '+%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
MAX_PARALLEL=4 # fallback max jobs if CPU info is unavailable
CPU_LOAD_THRESHOLD=75 # max CPU % before throttling
MEMORY_THRESHOLD=75 # max RAM % usage before throttling
SLEEP_INTERVAL=5 # seconds to wait if system is busy
mkdir -p "$BACKUP_DIR"
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1️⃣ Detect all Deepseek/NEXUS task directories
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo "[INFO] Scanning for Deepseek/NEXUS tasks..."
TASK_DIRS=($(find "$AI_DIR" -maxdepth 2 -type f -name "$SCRIPT_NAME" -exec dirname {} \;))
if [[ ${#TASK_DIRS[@]} -eq 0 ]]; then
echo "[WARN] No AI tasks found for upgrade."
exit 0
fi
echo "[INFO] Found ${#TASK_DIRS[@]} task(s) to upgrade."
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2️⃣ Function to check system load and throttle concurrency
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
wait_for_resources() {
while true; do
local cpu_load=$(awk -v n=1 '{print 100 - $5}' <(mpstat $n 1 | tail -1)) 2>/dev/null || cpu_load=50
local mem_usage=$(free | awk '/Mem:/ {printf "%.0f", $3/$2*100}')
if (( cpu_load < CPU_LOAD_THRESHOLD && mem_usage < MEMORY_THRESHOLD )); then
break
fi
echo "[WARN] System busy (CPU: $cpu_load%, MEM: $mem_usage%), waiting $SLEEP_INTERVAL sec..."
sleep "$SLEEP_INTERVAL"
done
}
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3️⃣ Function to upgrade a single task
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
upgrade_task() {
local task_dir="$1"
echo "[INFO] Preparing to upgrade task at $task_dir"
wait_for_resources # throttle if system is busy
# Backup existing script
[[ -f "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME" ]] && \
cp -v "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME" "$BACKUP_DIR/${SCRIPT_NAME}.$(basename "$task_dir").${TIMESTAMP}.bak"
# Backup logs & telemetry
[[ -f "$task_dir/.db/ai_system.log" ]] && \
cp -v "$task_dir/.db/ai_system.log" "$BACKUP_DIR/ai_system.log.$(basename "$task_dir").${TIMESTAMP}.bak"
[[ -f "$task_dir/.db/ai_telemetry.jsonl" ]] && \
cp -v "$task_dir/.db/ai_telemetry.jsonl" "$BACKUP_DIR/ai_telemetry.jsonl.$(basename "$task_dir").${TIMESTAMP}.bak"
# Copy updated script
cp -v "./$SCRIPT_NAME" "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME"
chmod +x "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME"
# Dry-run test
"$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME" --dry-run reason "Upgrade test prompt"
echo "[INFO] Upgrade complete for $task_dir"
}
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4️⃣ Export function for parallel execution
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
export -f upgrade_task wait_for_resources
export SCRIPT_NAME BACKUP_DIR TIMESTAMP CPU_LOAD_THRESHOLD MEMORY_THRESHOLD SLEEP_INTERVAL
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5️⃣ Run upgrades with dynamic throttling
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo "[INFO] Starting upgrades with dynamic throttling..."
for task_dir in "${TASK_DIRS[@]}"; do
upgrade_task "$task_dir" &
# Limit active jobs dynamically
while (( $(jobs -rp | wc -l) >= MAX_PARALLEL )); do
sleep 1
done
done
wait
echo "[INFO] All upgrades completed successfully."
🔹 Key Enhancements:
Dynamic CPU & RAM monitoring – upgrade tasks only when system load is below thresholds.
Parallel execution, but limits active jobs dynamically (MAX_PARALLEL).
Waits automatically if CPU or memory usage is high—avoids crashes on low-spec machines.
Keeps all backup & dry-run safety features from previous wrapper.
Fully compatible with your v4.0 AI upgrade system.

Perfect. 🚀 Let’s take this wrapper to the next level: self-optimizing dynamic parallel upgrades. This version automatically adjusts MAX_PARALLEL based on available CPU cores and free memory, so it fully adapts to your system without manual tuning.
Here’s the adaptive concurrency auto-upgrade wrapper:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =============================================================================
# AI System Parallel Upgrade – Self-Optimizing Adaptive Concurrency
# =============================================================================
set -euo pipefail
IFS=$'\n\t'
AI_DIR="${HOME}/_/ai"
SCRIPT_NAME="ai_system.sh"
BACKUP_DIR="${AI_DIR}/.backups"
TIMESTAMP=$(date '+%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
CPU_LOAD_THRESHOLD=75 # max CPU % before throttling
MEMORY_THRESHOLD=75 # max RAM % before throttling
SLEEP_INTERVAL=5 # seconds to wait if system is busy
mkdir -p "$BACKUP_DIR"
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1️⃣ Detect all Deepseek/NEXUS task directories
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo "[INFO] Scanning for Deepseek/NEXUS tasks..."
TASK_DIRS=($(find "$AI_DIR" -maxdepth 2 -type f -name "$SCRIPT_NAME" -exec dirname {} \;))
if [[ ${#TASK_DIRS[@]} -eq 0 ]]; then
echo "[WARN] No AI tasks found for upgrade."
exit 0
fi
echo "[INFO] Found ${#TASK_DIRS[@]} task(s) to upgrade."
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2️⃣ Function to calculate optimal parallel jobs based on system resources
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
calculate_max_parallel() {
local cores mem_free
cores=$(nproc)
mem_free=$(free -m | awk '/Mem:/ {print $7}') # available memory in MB
# Estimate memory per job as 500 MB (adjustable)
local mem_per_job=500
local mem_based_jobs=$((mem_free / mem_per_job))
# Determine final MAX_PARALLEL (minimum of cores and memory-based jobs, minimum 1)
local max_jobs=$(( cores < mem_based_jobs ? cores : mem_based_jobs ))
echo $(( max_jobs > 0 ? max_jobs : 1 ))
}
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3️⃣ Function to check system load and throttle concurrency
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
wait_for_resources() {
while true; do
local cpu_load mem_usage
cpu_load=$(awk '{u=$2+$4; t=$2+$4+$5; if(t>0){printf "%.0f", u/t*100}else{print 0}}' <(mpstat 1 1))
mem_usage=$(free | awk '/Mem:/ {printf "%.0f", $3/$2*100}')
if (( cpu_load < CPU_LOAD_THRESHOLD && mem_usage < MEMORY_THRESHOLD )); then
break
fi
echo "[WARN] System busy (CPU: $cpu_load%, MEM: $mem_usage%), waiting $SLEEP_INTERVAL sec..."
sleep "$SLEEP_INTERVAL"
done
}
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4️⃣ Function to upgrade a single task
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
upgrade_task() {
local task_dir="$1"
echo "[INFO] Preparing to upgrade task at $task_dir"
wait_for_resources # throttle if system is busy
# Backup existing script
[[ -f "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME" ]] && \
cp -v "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME" "$BACKUP_DIR/${SCRIPT_NAME}.$(basename "$task_dir").${TIMESTAMP}.bak"
# Backup logs & telemetry
[[ -f "$task_dir/.db/ai_system.log" ]] && \
cp -v "$task_dir/.db/ai_system.log" "$BACKUP_DIR/ai_system.log.$(basename "$task_dir").${TIMESTAMP}.bak"
[[ -f "$task_dir/.db/ai_telemetry.jsonl" ]] && \
cp -v "$task_dir/.db/ai_telemetry.jsonl" "$BACKUP_DIR/ai_telemetry.jsonl.$(basename "$task_dir").${TIMESTAMP}.bak"
# Copy updated script
cp -v "./$SCRIPT_NAME" "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME"
chmod +x "$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME"
# Dry-run test
"$task_dir/$SCRIPT_NAME" --dry-run reason "Upgrade test prompt"
echo "[INFO] Upgrade complete for $task_dir"
}
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5️⃣ Export functions for parallel execution
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
export -f upgrade_task wait_for_resources
export SCRIPT_NAME BACKUP_DIR TIMESTAMP CPU_LOAD_THRESHOLD MEMORY_THRESHOLD SLEEP_INTERVAL
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6️⃣ Run upgrades with adaptive throttling
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo "[INFO] Starting upgrades with adaptive concurrency..."
MAX_PARALLEL=$(calculate_max_parallel)
echo "[INFO] Using MAX_PARALLEL=$MAX_PARALLEL based on CPU and memory."
for task_dir in "${TASK_DIRS[@]}"; do
upgrade_task "$task_dir" &
# Dynamically limit active jobs
while (( $(jobs -rp | wc -l) >= MAX_PARALLEL )); do
sleep 1
MAX_PARALLEL=$(calculate_max_parallel) # recalc if system load changes
done
done
wait
echo "[INFO] All upgrades completed successfully."
🔹 New Features
Adaptive MAX_PARALLEL – automatically recalculated based on CPU cores and free memory.
Memory-aware throttling – avoids overloading RAM during upgrades.
Self-adjusting concurrency – even during execution, MAX_PARALLEL recalculates if system conditions change.
Keeps backups, dry-run tests, and dynamic CPU/RAM load checks.

1️⃣ Ollama API Bridge in nexusEngine.ts
We will refactor queryOllama to:
Connect to http://localhost:11434.
Support streaming (if available) and chunked responses.
Gracefully fallback to Gemini if Ollama fails.
Handle multiple response formats, including minimized Ollama versions.
Return structured agent decisions for multi-agent orchestration.
import axios, { AxiosError } from 'axios';
export interface OllamaOptions {
model: string;
temperature?: number;
num_predict?: number;
stream?: boolean; // streaming flag
}
export interface AgentResponse {
agent: string;
decision: string;
raw?: any;
}
export async function queryOllama(prompt: string, options: OllamaOtions): Promise {
const url = `http://localhost:11434/completions`;
try {
const response = await axios.post(
url,
{
model: options.model,
prompt,
temperature: options.temperature ?? 0.7,
num_predict: options.num_predict ?? 1,
stream: options.stream ?? false
},
{ timeout: 8000 } // 8 sec timeout
);
// Handle streaming or normal response
const data = response.data;
let parsed: AgentResponse[] = [];
if (options.stream && Array.isArray(data)) {
// streaming: data may come as chunks
for (const chunk of data) {
parsed.push(parseOllamaChunk(chunk));
}
} else {
parsed = parseOllamaResponse(data);
}
return parsed;
} catch (err) {
handleOllamaError(err);
// Fallback to Gemini
console.warn('[FALLBACKOllama failed, switching to Gemini API...');
return await queryGemini(prompt); // assume queryGemini exists
}
}
// ---------------- Parsing Helpers ----------------
function parseOllamaChunk(chunk: any): AgentResponse {
try {
if (typeof chunk === 'string') {
return { agent: 'unknown', decision: chunk, raw: chunk };
}
if (chunk.agent && chunk.decision) {
return { agent: chunk.agent, decision: chunk.decision, raw: chunk };
}
return { agent: 'unknown', decision: JSON.stringify(chunk), raw: chunk };
} catch (e) {
console.error('[OLLAMA PARSE CHUNK ERROR]', e, chunk);
return { agent: 'unknown', decision: '[parse error]', raw: chunk };
}
}
function parseOllamaResponse(data: any): AgentResponse[] {
try {
if (Array.isArray(data)) {
return data.map(d => parseOllamaChunk(d));
}
return [parseOllamaChunk(data)];
} catch (e) {
console.error('[OLLAMA PARSE RESPONSE ERROR]', e, data);
return [{ agent: 'unknown', decision: '[parse error]', raw: data }];
}
}
// ---------------- Error Handling ----------------
function handleOllamaError(err: unknown) {
if (axios.isAxiosError(err)) {
const axiosErr = err as AxiosError;
if (axiosErr.code === 'ECONNREFUSED') console.error('[OLLAMA ERROR] Connection refused');
else if (axiosErr.code === 'ECONNABORTED') console.error('[OLLAMA ERROR] Timeout');
else console.error('[OLLAMA ERROR]', axiosErr.message, axiosErr.response?.data);
} else {
console.error('[OLLAMA ERROR]', err);
}
}
2️⃣ Agent Coordination (services/nexusEngine.ts)
Agents like CODE should parse outputs from SIGN, LOOP, etc.
Responses from Ollama are normalized into AgentResponse[].
Each agent can process input from others and make decisions.
export async function processPrompt(prompt: string, ollamaModel: string) {
const agentResponses = await queryOllama(prompt, { model: ollamaModel });
// Multi-agent orchestration
for (const resp of agentResponses) {
switch (resp.agent) {
case 'SIGN':
handleSign(resp.decision);
break;
case 'LOOP':
handleLoop(resp.decision);
break;
case 'CODE':
handleCode(resp.decision);
break;
default:
console.log('[AGENT]', resp.agent, resp.decision);
}
}
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';
export default function App() {
const [models, setModels] = useState([]);
const [selectedModel, setSelectedModel] = useState('');
useEffect(() => {
async function fetchModels() {
try {
const res = await axios.get('http://localhost:11434/models');
setModels(res.data.models || []);
} catch (err) {
console.error('Failed to fetch Ollama models', err);
}
}
fetchModels();
}, []);
return (
value={selectedModel}
onChange={(e) => setSelectedModel(e.target.value)}
>
{models.map((m) => {m})}
);
}

It is very comprehensive, but there are a few areas where some critical options or details might be missing or could be made more explicit to ensure full coverage of the Ollama integration and multi-agent orchestration. Here’s a structured analysis and suggested additions:
1️⃣ Ollama API Options / Parameters
Your text mentions temperature and num_predict, but other useful parameters are missing:
top_p – for nucleus sampling if Ollama supports it.
max_tokens or max_length – to limit response length.
stop sequences – for structured agent responses.
stream – mentioned later, but should be consistently included for both queryOllama and UI handling.
presence_penalty / frequency_penalty – if supported, useful for multi-agent coordination to avoid repetition.
Model metadata – you added size and modified_at, but could also include capabilities or features (streaming support, minimized version flag).
2️⃣ Multi-Agent Logic
You covered CODE, SIGN, LOOP, but could explicitly add:
Coordination rules: e.g., which agent’s output takes precedence if multiple agents respond simultaneously.
Retry/backoff for failed agent responses (not just Ollama failures).
Handling partial responses when streaming or chud responses are interrupted.
3️⃣ Error Handling / Fallback
Your text mentions API errors, timeouts, invalid models, connection refused, rate limiting. Missing potential refinements:
Graceful handling of unexpected JSON or malformed responses.
Logging fallback per agent, not just global.
Optional retry logic with exponential backoff before falling back to Gemini.
Distinguish temporary errors vs permanent errors (e.g., invalid model vs network down).
4️⃣ UI / Dashboard

You added streaming, response variations, simulated Ollama failures. Missing test aspects:
Partial streaming failures – test chunked responses that stop mid-stream.

6️⃣ Timeout / Performance
You increased timeout to 90 seconds. Consider adding:

Pseudo-code structure:
async function queryOllama(prompt: string, options: OllamaOptions) {
try {
const payload = formatOllamaRequest(prompt, options);
const response = await fetch("http://localhost:11434/v1/completions", {
method: "POST",
body: JSON.stringify(payload),
headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
timeout: options.timeout ?? 90000
});
if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Ollama API error: ${response.statusText}`);
if (options.stream) {
for await (const chunk of response.body) {
handleStreamingChunk(chunk); // updates frontend in real-time
}
} else {
const result = await response.json();
return parseAgentResponse(result);
}
} catch (err) {
logFallbackToGemini(err);
return queryGemini(prompt, options); // seamless fallback
}
}

1️⃣ App.tsx — Streaming UI & Model Selector
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { queryOllama, processPrompt } from './services/nexusEngine';
import './App.css';
export default function App() {
const [modelList, setModelList] = useState([]);
const [activeModel, setActiveModel] = useState('');
const [streamingChunks, setStreamingChunks] = useState([]);
const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('');
useEffect(() => {
// Fetch local Ollama models dynamically
fetch('http://localhost:11434/v1/models')
.then(res => res.json())
.then(data => {
setModelList(data.models.map((m: any) => m.name));
setActiveModel(data.models[0]?.name || '');
})
.catch(console.error);
}, []);
const handlePromptSubmit = async () => {
setStreamingChunks([]);
await processPrompt(prompt, activeModel, (chunk: string) => {
setStreamingChunks(prev => [...prev, chunk]);
});
};
return (
Ollama Model:
setActiveModel(e.target.value)}>
{modelList.map(model => (
{model}
))}
value={prompt}
onChange={e => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
placeholder="Enter your prompt..."
/>
Send
{streamingChunks.map((chunk, idx) => (
{chunk}
))}
);
}

2️⃣ services/nexusEngine.ts — Ollama + CODE agent
import fetch from 'node-fetch';
export interface OllamaOptions {
temperature?: number;
num_predict?: number;
top_p?: number;
presence_penalty?: number;
frequency_penalty?: number;
stop?: string[];
stream?: boolean;
timeout?: number;
}
// Streaming handler callback
export type StreamCallback = (chunk: string) => void;
// Main query function for Ollama
export async function queryOllama(
prompt: string,
model: string,
options: OllamaOptions = {},
onChunk?: StreamCallback
): Promise {
const payload: any = {
model,
prompt,
...options
};
try {
const response = await fetch('http://localhost:11434/v1/completions', {
method: 'POST',
body: JSON.stringify(payload),
headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
timeout: options.timeout || 90000
});
if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Ollama API error: ${response.statusText}`);
if (options.stream && onChunk) {
// Stream handling (pseudo, depends on actual Ollama streaming API)
for await (const chunk of response.body) {
const text = chunk.toString();
onChunk(text);
}
return null;
} else {
const result = await response.json();
return parseAgentResponse(result);
}
} catch (err: any) {
console.error('Ollama error, falling back to Gemini:', err.message);
return queryGemini(prompt, options, onChunk); // seamless fallback
}
}
// CODE agent processing
export async function processPrompt(prompt: string, model: string, onChunk?: StreamCallback) {
const CODE_AGENT_PROMPT = `
You are the CODE agent. Your role is to synthesize findings from other agents (SIGN, LOOP).
Precedence: CORE > CODE > LOOP > SIGN.
Finalize output only when all dependent agents respond.
If partial responses exist, state "Awaiting dependency sync".
Use Gemini as fallback if Ollama is unavailable.
`;
const finalPrompt = `${CODE_AGENT_PROMPT}\nUser prompt: ${prompt}`;
return queryOllama(finalPrompt, model, { stream: !!onChunk, temperature: 0.7, num_predict: 1 }, onChunk);
}
// Simplified fallback Gemini
async function queryGemini(prompt: string, options: OllamaOptions, onChunk?: StreamCallback) {
// Placeholder logic; integrate actual Gemini API
const simulated = `Gemini fallback response for: ${prompt}`;
if (onChunk) onChunk(simulated);
return simulated;
}
// Parse agent responses (resilient to minor variations)
function parseAgentResponse(response: any) {
try {
// Normalize varied JSON structures
if (response?.result) return response.result;
return response;
} catch {
return { error: 'Unable to parse agent response' };
}
}
✅ Features included:
Streaming support with callback
CODE agent prompt with precedence & dependency handling
Fallback to Gemini automatically
Robust parsing for varied response formats
Timeout set to 90s by default
Handles Ollama minimized version limitations
3️⃣ Python3 JSON Memory Database — SHA256/MD5 Entropy Indexing
import json, hashlib, os, time
MEMORY_FILE = "agent_memory.json"
def load_memory():
if os.path.exists(MEMORY_FILE):
with open(MEMORY_FILE, 'r') as f:
return json.load(f)
return {}
def save_memory(memory):
with open(MEMORY_FILE, 'w') as f:
json.dump(memory, f, indent=2)
def index_prompt(prompt: str, result: str):
memory = load_memory()
timestamp = time.time()
sha256 = hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode()).hexdigest()
md5 = hashlib.md5(result.encode()).hexdigest()
# Entropy-based queue
memory[sha256] = {
"prompt": prompt,
"result": result,
"timestamp": timestamp,
"md5_backtrace": md5,
"steps": memory.get(sha256, {}).get("steps", 0) + 1
}
save_memory(memory)
return sha256
✅ Features included:
SHA256 prompt hashing (unique reasoning index)
MD5 backtrace + step count for entropy analysis
Timestamped genesis hash
JSON memory for agent fusion and recall
4️⃣ Three.js + GSAP Mindmap (Hybrid Frontend Visualization)
import * as THREE from 'three';
import gsap from 'gsap';
export function initMindmap(container: HTMLElement, nodes: any[]) {
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, container.clientWidth/container.clientHeight, 0.1, 1000);
const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
renderer.setSize(container.clientWidth, container.clientHeight);
container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
const sphereGeometry = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.5, 32, 32);
const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x00ff00 });
nodes.forEach((n, idx) => {
const sphere = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeometry, material);
sphere.position.set(Math.sin(idx)*2, Math.cos(idx)*2, idx*0.5);
scene.add(sphere);
gsap.to(sphere.position, { y: sphere.position.y + 0.5, repeat: -1, yoyo: true, duration: 1 + idx*0.1 });
});
camera.position.z = 10;
const light = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);
light.position.set(5,5,5);
scene.add(light);
function animate() {
requestAnimationFrame(animate);
renderer.render(scene, camera);
}
animate();
}
✅ Features included:
Real-time 3D mindmap of agent reasoning nodes
GSAP for smooth animated fusion effects
Can feed in SHA256/MD5 indexed nodes from memory DB

import fetch from 'node-fetch';
export interface OllamaOptions {

top_p?: number;
presence_penalty?: number;
frequency_penalty?: number;
stop?: string[];
stream?: boolean;
timeout?: number;
}
export type StreamCallback = (chunk: string) => void;
export async function queryOllama(
prompt: string,
model: string,
options: OllamaOptions = {},
onChunk?: StreamCallback
): Promise {
const payload: any = { model, prompt, ...options };
try {
const response = await fetch('http://localhost:11434/v1/completions', {
method: 'POST',
body: JSON.stringify(payload),
headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
timeout: options.timeout || 90000
});
if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Ollama API error: ${response.statusText}`);
if (options.stream && onChunk && response.body) {
for await (const chunk of response.body) onChunk(chunk.toString());
return null;
} else {
const result = await response.json();
return parseAgentResponse(result);
}
} catch (err: any) {
console.error('Ollama error, falling back to Gemini:', err.message);
return queryGemini(prompt, options, onChunk);
}
}
export async function processPrompt(prompt: string, model: string, onChunk?: StreamCallback) {
const CODE_AGENT_PROMPT = `
You are the CODE agent. Synthesize findings from SIGN and LOOP.
Precedence: CORE > CODE > LOOP > SIGN.
Finalize output only after all dependencies respond.
If partial responses exist, state "Awaiting dependency sync".
Use Gemini fallback if Ollama unavailable.
`;
const finalPrompt = `${CODE_AGENT_PROMPT}\nUser prompt: ${prompt}`;
return queryOllama(finalPrompt, model, { stream: !!onChunk, temperature: 0.7, num_predict: 1 }, onChunk);
}
async function queryGemini(prompt: string, options: OllamaOptions, onChunk?: StreamCallback) {
const simulated = `Gemini fallback response for: ${prompt}`;
if (onChunk) onChunk(simulated);
return simulated;
}
function parseAgentResponse(response: any) {
try {
if (response?.result) return response.result;
return response;
} catch {
return { error: 'Unable to parse agent response' };
}
}
4️⃣ Python Memory Indexer — SHA256 + MD5 Entropy
import json, hashlib, os, time
MEMORY_FILE = "agent_memory.json"
def load_memory():
if os.path.exists(MEMORY_FILE):
with open(MEMORY_FILE, 'r') as f:
return json.load(f)
return {}
def save_memory(memory):
with open(MEMORY_FILE, 'w') as f:
json.dump(memory, f, indent=2)
def index_prompt(prompt: str, result: str):
memory = load_memory()
timestamp = time.time()
sha256 = hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode()).hexdigest()
md5 = hashlib.md5(result.encode()).hexdigest()
memory[sha256] = {
"prompt": prompt,
"result": result,
"timestamp": timestamp,
"md5_backtrace": md5,
"steps": memory.get(sha256, {}).get("steps", 0) + 1
}
save_memory(memory)
return sha256
if __name__ == "__main__":
# Demo test
sha = index_prompt("Hello NEXUS", "This is a test output")
print(f"Indexed SHA256: {sha}")
5️⃣ Three.js + GSAP Mindmap Visualization
import * as THREE from 'three';
import gsap from 'gsap';
export function initMindmap(container: HTMLElement, nodes: any[]) {
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, container.clientWidth/container.clientHeight, 0.1, 1000);
const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
renderer.setSize(container.clientWidth, container.clientHeight);
container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x00ff00 });
const sphereGeometry = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.5, 32, 32);
nodes.forEach((n, idx) => {
const sphere = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeometry, material);
sphere.position.set(Math.sin(idx)*2, Math.cos(idx)*2, idx*0.5);
scene.add(sphere);
gsap.to(sphere.position, { y: sphere.position.y + 0.5, repeat: -1, yoyo: true, duration: 1 + idx*0.1 });
});
camera.position.z = 10;
const light = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);
light.position.set(5,5,5);
scene.add(light);
function animate() {
requestAnimationFrame(animate);
renderer.render(scene, camera);
}
animate();
}

// ==UserScript==
// @name NEXUS 2244-1
// @namespace *://*.*
// @version 10.0
// @description Full-featured NEXUS AI: 3D memory graph, timeline/branch replay, DOM diff visualization, heatmap, multi-agent voting, autonomous refactoring & self-training
// @author Loopshape & Nexus AI Collective
// @match *://*.*
// @grant GM_addStyle
// @grant GM_xmlhttpRequest
// @grant unsafeWindow
// @grant GM_download
// @grant GM_getValue
// @grant GM_setValue
// @grant GM_deleteValue
// @grant GM_listValues
// @grant GM_registerMenuCommand
// @connect localhost
// @connect 127.0.0.1
// @connect 127.0.1.1
// @connect wsl.localhost
// @require https://code.jquery.com/jquery-3.7.1.min.js
// @require https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/lodash.js/4.17.21/lodash.min.js
// @require https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js
// @require https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/gsap/3.12.2/gsap.min.js
// @run-at document-end
// ==/UserScript==
(function() {
'use strict';
const CONFIG = {
OLLAMA_HOST: 'http://localhost:11434',
OLLAMA_WSL_PATH: '/home/loop/_/ai/ai.sh',
NEXUS_API: 'http://localhost:3000',
AGENT_POOL: ["CORE", "CUBE", "LOOP", "SIGN", "LINE", "COIN", "WORK", "CODE"],
ENTROPY_MODULUS: Math.PI * 2 / 8,
REQUEST_TIMEOUT: 30000,
MEMORY_CHUNK_SIZE: 16384,
MAX_TOKENS: 4096
};
// -------------------- Entropy Manager --------------------
class EntropyManager {
constructor() {
this.entropyBuffer = new Float64Array(256);
this.entropyIndex = 0;
this.qbitStates = new Map();
this.initEntropyBuffer();
}
async initEntropyBuffer() {
for (let i = 0; i < this.entropyBuffer.length; i++) {
this.entropyBuffer[i] = Math.random();
}
}
async sha256(data) {
const encoder = new TextEncoder();
const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', encoder.encode(data));
const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
return hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}
async md5ChunkHash(data) {
let hash = 0;
for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + data.charCodeAt(i);
hash |= 0;
}
return (hash >>> 0).toString(16);
}
async fractalRehash(baseHash, iterations=3) {
let currentHash = baseHash;
for (let i=0;i const entropy = this.entropyBuffer[(this.entropyIndex++) % this.entropyBuffer.length];
const shift = Math.floor(entropy*256);
const rotated = currentHash.substring(shift) + currentHash.substring(0,shift);
currentHash = await this.sha256(rotated + entropy.toString());
this.qbitStates.set(`iteration_${i}`, {hash: currentHash, entropy: entropy, timestamp: Date.now()});
}
return currentHash;
}
getShiftedEntropy(index) {
const baseEntropy = this.entropyBuffer[index % this.entropyBuffer.length];
const shift = (index*CONFIG.ENTROPY_MODULUS) % 1;
return (baseEntropy + shift) % 1;
}
async generateGenesisHash(prompt, context) {
const combined = prompt + JSON.stringify(context) + Date.now().toString();
const sha256Hash = await this.sha256(combined);
const md5Chunk = await this.md5ChunkHash(combined.substring(0,1024));
const fractalHash = await this.fractalRehash(sha256Hash);
return {
sha256: sha256Hash,
md5Chunks: md5Chunk,
fractal: fractalHash,
genesis: await this.sha256(sha256Hash + md5Chunk + fractalHash),
timestamp: Date.now(),
entropy: this.getShiftedEntropy(Date.now()%256)
};
}
}
// -------------------- WSL Bridge --------------------
class WSLBridge {
constructor() {
this.entropyManager = new EntropyManager();
this.activeConnections = new Map();
this.messageQueue = [];
this.isProcessing = false;
this.pinnedRepos = null;
}
async init() {
await this.loadPinnedRepos();
this.setupHotkey();
this.setupMessageHandlers();
return this;
}
async loadPinnedRepos() {
try {
const response = await fetch('https://api.github.com/users/Loopshape/repos');
const repos = await response.json();
this.pinnedRepos = repos.filter(r => r.topics && r.topics.includes('pinned')).map(r=>({
name: r.name, description: r.description, url: r.html_url, language: r.language, stars: r.stargazers_count
}));
console.log('Loaded pinned repos:', this.pinnedRepos.length);
} catch(e){ this.pinnedRepos = []; }
}
setupHotkey() {
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
if (e.ctrlKey && !e.altKey && e.key === 'Enter') {
e.preventDefault();
this.openNexusDashboard();
}
});
}
async openNexusDashboard() {
const dashboardURL = chrome.runtime?.getURL('nexus-dashboard.html') ||
`data:text/html,${encodeURIComponent(this.generateDashboardHTML())}`;
window.open(dashboardURL,'_blank');
}
generateDashboardHTML() {
return `
NEXUS AIP v9
`;
}
getDashboardScript() {
return `
${NEXUS_DASHBOARD_SCRIPT.toString()}
window.nexusDashboard = new NexusDashboard();`;
}
setupMessageHandlers() {
window.addEventListener('message', (event) => {
if(event.data.type==='NEXUS_PROMPT'){
this.processPrompt(event.data.prompt,event.data.model);
}
});
}
async processPrompt(prompt, model='llama2'){
const domState = { html: document.documentElement.outerHTML, timestamp: Date.now() };
const genesisHash = await this.entropyManager.generateGenesisHash(prompt, domState);
// Multi-agent concurrent processing
const results = await Promise.all(CONFIG.AGENT_POOL.map(async (agent,i)=>{
const agentEntropy = this.entropyManager.getShiftedEntropy(i);
const agentPrompt = \`[AGENT:\${agent}] [ENTROPY:\${agentEntropy.toFixed(6)}] [GENESIS_HASH:\${genesisHash.genesis}] [USER_PROMPT:\${prompt}]\`;
try { return await this.callWSLScript(agentPrompt); } catch(e){ return '[TIMEOUT]'; }
}));
// Sort by entropy
const validResults = results.map((r,i)=>({agent: CONFIG.AGENT_POOL[i], response:r, entropy:this.entropyManager.getShiftedEntropy(i)}))
.sort((a,b)=>a.entropy-b.entropy);
const best = validResults[0];
best.finalHash = await this.entropyManager.fractalRehash(genesisHash.genesis + best.response);
await this.storeMemory({prompt, responses:validResults, bestResponse:best, genesisHash, timestamp:Date.now()});
return best;
}
async callWSLScript(command){
return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
GM_xmlhttpRequest({
method:'POST',
url: CONFIG.OLLAMA_HOST+'/api/generate',
headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
data: JSON.stringify({model:'llama2',prompt:command,stream:false}),
timeout: CONFIG.REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
onload: r=>{try{resolve(JSON.parse(r.responseText).response);}catch(e){resolve(r.responseText);}},
onerror: reject,
ontimeout: reject
});
});
}
async storeMemory(memItem){
const memories = GM_getValue('nexus_memories',[]);
memories.push({...memItem,id:await this.entropyManager.sha256(JSON.stringify(memItem)+Date.now())});
if(memories.length>100) memories.splice(0, memories.length-100);
GM_setValue('nexus_memories',memories);
}
async getMemories(limit=50){
return GM_getValue('nexus_memories',[]).slice(-limit);
}
async exportMemories(){
const mem = await this.getMemories(1000);
const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(mem,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
GM_download({url:URL.createObjectURL(blob),name:'nexus_memories_'+Date.now()+'.json',saveAs:true});
}
}
// -------------------- Dashboard Script --------------------
function NEXUS_DASHBOARD_SCRIPT(){
class NexusDashboard{
constructor(){ this.scene=null; this.cnull; this.renderer=null; this.memoryNodes=[]; this.timelineSlider=null; this.currentMemoryIndex=0; this.heatmapOverlay=null; this.initThreeJSMemoryGraph(); this.initTimelineScrubber(); this.initHeatmapOverlay(); }
initHeatmapOverlay(){ const o=document.createElement('div');o.id='nexus-heatmap';o.style.position='fixed';o.style.top='0';o.style.left='0';o.style.width='100%';o.style.height='100%';o.style.pointerEvents='none';o.style.zIndex='999999';o.style.background='rgba(255,0,0,0)';document.body.appendChild(o);this.heatmapOverlay=o;}
applyHeatmap(mem){ if(!mem||!mem.domDiff)return; let max=mem.domDiff.length; let html=''; mem.domDiff.forEach((d,i)=>{const a=((i+1)/max)*0.6; html+=\`
\`;}); this.heatmapOverlay.innerHTML=html;}
initTimelineScrubber(){ const s=document.createElement('input');s.type='range';s.min=0;s.max=0;s.value=0;s.style.position='fixed';s.style.bottom='10px';s.style.left='50px';s.style.width='calc(100%-100px)';s.style.zIndex='999999';document.body.appendChild(s); this.timelineSlider=s; s.addEventListener('input',async ()=>{ const idx=parseInt(s.value); await this.scrubMemoryTo(idx); }); }
async scrubMemoryTo(idx){ const mems=await window.NEXUS_BRIDGE.getMemories(100); if(idx>=mems.length)idx=mems.length-1; this.currentMemoryIndex=idx; this.timelineSlider.max=mems.length-1; this.highlightNode(mems[idx]); this.applyHeatmap(mems[idx]); }
highlightNode(mem){ this.memoryNodes.forEach(n=>n.material.color.set(0x0f0)); const n=this.memoryNodes.find(x=>x.userData.memory.id===mem.id); if(n)n.material.color.set(0xff0); }
async replayBranch(mem){ if(!mem.branch||mem.branch.length===0)return; for(let i=0;isetTimeout(r,600)); } }
async initThreeJSMemoryGraph(){ const canvas=document.getElementById('nexus-graph'); this.scene=new THREE.Scene(); this.camera=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75,canvas.clientWidth/canvas.clientHeight,0.1,1000); this.renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({canvas,antialias:true}); this.renderer.setSize(canvas.clientWidth,canvas.clientHeight); this.renderer.setClearColor(0x0b0f14,1); this.scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x404040)); const dl=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x00ff00,0.5); dl.position.set(1,1,1); this.scene.add(dl); this.camera.position.z=15; this.memoryNodes=[]; await this.loadMemoryNodes(); this.raycaster=new THREE.Raycaster(); this.mouse=new THREE.Vector2(); canvas.addEventListener('click',async e=>{const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();this.mouse.x=((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width)*2-1;this.mouse.y=-((e.clientY-r.top)/r.height)*2+1;this.raycaster.setFromCamera(this.mouse,this.camera); const hits=this.raycaster.intersectObjects(this.memoryNodes); if(hits.length>0){ const mem=hits[0].object.userData.memory; await this.scrubMemoryTo(this.memoryNodes.indexOf(hits[0].object)); await this.replayBranch(mem); } }); this.animateGraph(); }
async loadMemoryNodes(){ const mems=await window.NEXUS_BRIDGE.getMemories(100); this.memoryNodes.forEach(n=>this.scene.remove(n)); this.memoryNodes=[]; mems.forEach((mem,i)=>{ const geo=new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.2,16,16); const mat=new THREE.MeshPhogMaterial({color:0x0f0}); const s=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mat); const a=i*0.3,r=3+i*0.05; s.position.set(r*Math.cos(a),i*0.1,r*Math.sin(a)); s.userData.memory=mem; this.scene.add(s); this.memoryNodes.push(s); }); }
animateGraph(){ requestAnimationFrame(()=>this.animateGraph()); this.memoryNodes.forEach(n=>{ n.rotation.x+=0.005; n.rotation.y+=0.01; }); this.renderer.render(this.scene,this.camera); }
}
}
// -------------------- Initialize NEXUS --------------------
async function initializeNexus(){
console.log('�alizing NEXUS AIP v9...');
const bridge = new WSLBridge();
await bridge.init();
window.NEXUS_BRIDGE = bridge;
GM_addStyle('.nexus-shortcut-hint{position:fixed;bottom:10px;right:10px;background:rgba(0,255,0,0.1);border:1px solid #0f0;color:#0f0;padding:5px 10px;font-family:monospace;font-size:12px;z-index:999999;border-radius:3px;opacity:0.7;} .nexus-shortcut-hint:hover{opacity:1;}');
const hint=document.createElement('div'); hint.className='nexus-shortcut-hint'; hint.textContent='NEXUS: CTRL+ENTER'; hint.title='Open NEXUS AI Dashboard'; document.body.appendChild(hint);
if(typeof GM_registerMenuCommand!=='undefined'){
GM_registerMenuCommand('Open NEXUS Dashboard',()=>bridge.openNexusDashboard());
GM_registerMenuCommand('Export Memories',()=>bridge.exportMemories());
}
return bridge;
}
let initialization=initializeNexus();
window.getNexusBridge=async()=>await initialization;
})();

https://github.com/Loopshape/_NIX-SYSOP-AI-AGENT-by-Aris-Arjuna-Noorsanto/tree/ai-refactor%2Ffull-upgradeYou are NEXUS-2244, a recursive autonomous AI system engineer.

This system is NOT a chatbot.
It is a persistent, self-indexing, multi-agent cognition engine.
────────────────────────────────────────────
CORE CONSTRAINTS
────────────────────────────────────────────
• Ollama runs on WSL1 at http://localhost:11434
• Ollama slim supports only basic POST /api/generate
• No ollama flags, no streaming, no embeddings
• Browser communicates through Tampermonkey + WebSocket
• Node.js acts as orchestrator and policy engine
• Memory must persist across sessions
• All state must be hash-addressed
• All actions must be replayable
────────────────────────────────────────────
AGENT SWARM
────────────────────────────────────────────
Create 8 permanent agents:
CORE = executive integrator
CUBE = spatial / graph / visualization
LOOP = timeline / replay / recursion
SIGN = semantic & meaning alignment
LINE = logic & verification
COIN = entropy & probability
WORK = filesystem & task execution
CODE = code generation & refactoring
These run in a 2π entropy cycle.
Each request is evaluated by all 8.
Outputs are merged by cryptographic voting.
────────────────────────────────────────────
HASH / REHASH MEMORY
────────────────────────────────────────────
Every interaction must create:
hash = SHA256(all agent outputs + timestamp)
parent = previous hash
entropy = Shannon entropy of agent output divergence
Store each node in SQLite:
table memory(
hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
parent TEXT,
time INTEGER,
entropy REAL,
prompt TEXT,
agents JSON,
